The `four_total` plus `coin_add_or_sub` flag is true, and the other-four flag is true. R2 clock is 117/160 versus 115/160. That allowance flag is true. Wilson intervals and the pool counts are in the clock block.
     6

     9	The language screen on this adapter passes. GSM8K is 364/400 versus reason-s1 369/400, program rate 0.0. MMLU is 781/1140 versus 740/1140. WikiText perplexity is 19.279 versus reason-s1 13.817. Pinned answers on the six families are 700/768 versus 733/768. G1 accepts 511/512, matching reason-s1, and 8/8 long runs are exact. Compile held-out is 41/88. Addition exact on positive items is 148/160 versus reason-s1 150/160.
    10	
    11	The last logged counted-SFT step is 399, with pinned accuracy 1.0 and Mealy accuracy 1.0. Sixteen transcripts are in `research/r4/results/transcripts.json`.
    12	
    13	## Decisions
    14	
    15	These change what is trained. They are recorded here so a later result cannot quietly revise them.
    16	
    17	- Sentence mode stays out. SEMANTIC.md §6.2 says the pilot is not clearly positive: held-out templates were 1/18, and the line skipped its counted eval.
    18	- RegVM stays out. A regvm call is a third program form (`machine: regvm`). The program-writing pilot was revised after seeing which rules failed. That is not a simple addition to REASON's calls.
    19	- There is no `events:` alphabet line. SEMANTIC_FORMAT already uses `events:` for sentence spans. The alphabet is taught by compile's families and by the walk error, which names the table's events.
    20	- `use` lines are in. `compile_core` expands them; a program without one parses as before (REASON_FORMAT §12).
    21	- `first: number k` / `second: number k` are in. Quotes and `result k` stay valid and stay in the training mix.
    22	- An identical resubmission is the same program block as an earlier failed call, with the same notes list-signature. A new comma-separated list line is a notes repair and is allowed. The rule is in REASON_FORMAT §12.
    23	- The eval generation budget stays 12,000 tokens per round, the harness value REASON published, so the comparison is paired.
    24	- R1 keeps parking and the other five held-out templates, including the clipped counters.
    25	- Frontier models are not re-run. The existing records in `research/reason/results/frontier/` stand.
    26	- Training and the long loop use H100. Evals that fit use L40S. L4 and A10G do not fit the 40,960-token loop.
    27	
    28	## Data
    29	
    30	Every training example is built through `reason_core.build` and kept only when the reference calls match Python.
    31	
    32	- REASON's composites, including the plain variant and the old typo/citation repairs (`error_frac` 0.10 of `reason` draws).
    33	- Layouts from `r4_data.layout_example`: interleaved operands, parenthetical readings, a unit before the number, and a quoted pair. Positional `number k` and chains of results.
    34	- Compile's generator, training cell `iid` only (`compile_gen.example` / `number_example`). Held-out cells are never drawn. Library form when the generator emits a `use` line.
    35	- Repairs: on-policy items from the dagger file when it exists (about 70% of repair draws), otherwise the synthetic faults (wrong alphabet, Moore header on a Mealy table, bare citation). The faulty call is masked. The repair sentence and the reference call are targets.
    36	- Executor: 16 pinned tables and 16 Mealy tables every step, a subtracter on 15% of the Mealy draws.
    37	- Language: ordinary 0.25, general 0.20, negarith 0.04, KL coefficient 1.0.
    38	
    39	The mix is `r4_train.REGISTERED_MIX`. Sentence draws use a max of 2,048 events, and 15% of them go to 4,096 (`--sentence_long_frac 0.15`).
    40	
    41	## SFT
    42	
    43	From `/reasonvol/reason-s1/adapter_step500`, merged fresh in the job. Counted run, after the pilot and after an amendment that states the accuracy predictions:
    44	
    45	- 400 steps, seed 1, checkpoints at 0.5 and 1.0 (steps 200 and 400)
    46	- learning rate 1e-4, cosine, warmup 20
    47	- 32,768 tokens per step, max example 28,000, microbatch 28,000
    48	- the registered mix, `--error_frac 0.10`, sentence lengths above
    49	
    50	The pilot is uncounted: about 60 steps, seed 555, same mix, before those predictions are written.
    51	
    52	## Selection (seed 101 only)
    53	
    54	The validation problems are fixed:
    55	
    56	```
    57	parking_fee:test:64,1024:4;drone_compass:test:64,1024:4;coin_add_or_sub:test:64,1024:4;four_total:test:10,400:4;rumor_chain:test:64,1024:4;marble_trades:test:64,1024:4
    58	```
    59	
    60	`make_problems` seeds each cell with `101|{template}|test|{L}`, so this is 48 problems and the same 48 for reason-s1 and for every r4 checkpoint. Report every checkpoint. Select the highest accuracy. A tie goes to the later step.
    61	
    62	Seed 777 is not used to select and is not used to decide whether RL runs.
    63

   281	
   282	## RL, registered before it can run
   283	
   284	Run only if both of these hold on the selected checkpoint, and the cap still covers a re-eval of R1 (clock) plus a language eval:
   285	
   286	1. Selected accuracy on the 48 is at least 0.08 above reason-s1 on those same 48.
   287	2. Language screen, read from `research/reason/results/lm-s1/lm_reason-s1-500.json` and the new `pb_lmeval` JSON: GSM8K rate at least the reason-s1 rate minus 0.04, GSM8K program rate at most 0.05, MMLU rate at least the reason-s1 rate minus 0.015.
   288	
   289	If the gate fails, the reported model is the selected SFT adapter.
   290	
   291	PROMPTBOX's summed learning rate is 60 steps at 1e-5, one Adam step each, constant: 6e-4 (`r4_reward.PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR`).
   292	
   293	- Arm B, the arm that runs if the gate passes: 80 steps, warmup 0, cosine as in `reason_train.lr_at`. `--lr` is `r4_reward.arm_b_lr()`, the value whose schedule sums to 15 times that. Executor terms and the KL rehearsal stay on every update.
   294	- Arm C, only if B finishes, B's language screen passes, and the cap still holds a re-eval: 40 steps at a constant 3e-4 (20 times). If B fails the language screen, Arm C does not run and RL is not adopted.
   295	
   296	Reward (`r4_reward.score`): 1 if the final answer is correct and the response ended; plus 0.25 times the fraction of reference rows present in the written tables (tape rows are (state, event, destination); number-mode rows are the 5-token row; 0 if the prompt has no reference table); minus 0.2 per `identical to call` error; minus 0.01 per 1,000 generated tokens; plus 0.1 if any call succeeds after an error.
   297

  1326	
  1327	## Counted eval (seed 777, after selection)
  1328	
  1329	Same cells and the same `make_problems` seed as REASON, so each problem is paired:
  1330	
  1331	- R1: `research/reason/jobs/cells.py` `HELD`, n = 16
  1332	- R2: `PHRASE`, n = 8
  1333	- the no-clock variant on those cells
  1334	- gates: six families, G1, addition, language (GSM8K, MMLU, WikiText)
  1335	- compile held-out, registered here before any compile eval: tape `tmpl`, `kind`, `style`, `all3` at 64 and 256 events, n = 4; number arm of those four cells at 8 and 40 digits, n = 4; legacy elevator, clock, pattern at 64 and 256, n = 4. Length 4,096 is not in this set.
  1336	
  1337	`r4_report.py` prints k, n and Wilson intervals from the result JSON. It checks the pooled k against the sum of the cells, against the records' `correct` flags, and against a second scorer (the saved answer region, the problem's full answer, `last_number` / `answer_region`). A report that does not match is not entered in this file.
  1338	
  1339	## Smoke, then the rest
  1340	
  1341	Uncounted, and read before any larger job of that type:
  1342	
  1343	- `smoke-train`: 4 steps, seed 555, short lengths, the registered mix, H100
  1344	- `smoke-loop`: reason-s1, two problems, seed 555, the new clock, L40S
  1345	
  1346	Then dagger (training prompts only, seed 11), the pilot, the prediction amendment, counted SFT, validation, the counted eval, and RL only under the gate.
  1347	
  1348	## Smoke (uncounted, seed 555)
  1349	
  1350	Read before any larger train or loop. The train record is `research/r4/results/smoke-train/train_record.json` (adapter `/vol/smoke-train/adapter_step4` on `polyformer-r4-results`). The loop used the 2,500-token smoke budget, so a response that stops on `length` is the smoke cap. The quote error names the repair, and a resubmitted program received `identical to call`. Counts below are `r4_report.py` on `research/r4/results/smoke-loop/smoke_reason.json`.

  1622	Predictions for the counted run. Not edited after seed 777 is opened.
  1623	
  1624	1. Seed 101, the 48 validation problems: the selected checkpoint is at least 0.04 above reason-s1 on those problems. The RL gate stays at 0.08 plus the language screen. Below 0.04, SFT did not clearly help and RL does not run.
  1625	2. Seed 777, R1 clock, the HELD cells, n = 16: r4's rate is at least reason-s1's rate on those cells plus 0.05. reason-s1's rate is the sum of the r1a and r1b records, read by `r4_report.py`.
  1626	3. Inside that R1, `four_total` and `coin_add_or_sub` pooled: r4 is at least 0.10 above reason-s1. `parking_fee`, `drone_compass`, `rumor_chain` and `marble_trades` pooled: r4 is no more than 0.08 below reason-s1.
  1627	4. Seed 777, R2 clock, the PHRASE cells, n = 8: r4 is no more than 0.05 below reason-s1.
  1628	5. The selected checkpoint passes the language screen already registered.
  1629	6. Pinned and Mealy accuracy in the counted train record are 1.0 at the last logged step.
  1630	
  1631	## What could be wrong
  1632	
  1633	1. A container running yesterday's sources. The image env `R4_SOURCES_SHA256` changes when the sources change, the worker overwrites `/root/pb` with the shipped files and refuses on a hash mismatch, and a job is spawned only from a clean commit. Ruled out for a job when its log shows the matching hash and the new error text.
  1634	2. A merged adapter left in `/tmp` by a warm container. The worker deletes `/tmp/pb_*merged*` and `reason_loop` merges to `/tmp/pb_reason_merged_{PB_JOB_ID}`. Ruled out when the job log shows a merge into that path.
  1635	3. A typed or unpaired count. `r4_report.py` is the only way a count enters this file, and the second scorer has to match. Comparisons use the same cells and the same seed. Ruled out per table when the script prints `"match": true`.
  1636	
  1637	Evidence. The counted jobs exited without a source-hash error. `research/r4/results/r4-r1a/job.log.json` records the merge path `/tmp/pb_reason_merged_r4-r1a`. The clock, no-clock, compile, family, G1, and addition blocks above all have `"match": true`.

     1	"""
     2	Numbers for R4.md. Every reported count is read from a results JSON. The summary's k is checked against the
     3	sum of the cells and against a second scorer over each record's `got` and `answer`.
     4	
     5	    python research/r4/r4_report.py research/reason/results/r1a-clock/r1a_reason.json
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import json
    11	import re
    12	import sys
    13	import pathlib
    14	
    15	sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "promptbox"))
    16	from pb_common import interval_record  # noqa: E402
    17	
    18	
    19	def _num(text):
    20	    s = str(text).strip()
    21	    return s.lstrip("-").isdigit()
    22	
    23	
    24	def _problems(path):
    25	    directory = pathlib.Path(path).parent
    26	    cands = sorted(directory.glob("*problems.json"))
    27	    for cand in cands:
    28	        doc = json.loads(cand.read_text())
    29	        if isinstance(doc.get("problems"), list):
    30	            return doc["problems"], str(cand)
    31	    return None, None
    32	
    33	
    34	def rescore_pair(rec, prob, last_number, answer_region):
    35	    """Score the saved region against the problem's full answer. Does not read rec['correct']."""
    36	    if rec.get("finish") != "stop":
    37	        return False
    38	    text = rec.get("scored_region") or rec.get("response_tail") or ""
    39	    region, _ = answer_region(text)
    40	    kind = (prob or {}).get("answer_kind")
    41	    ans = (prob or {}).get("answer", rec.get("answer"))
    42	    ans = "" if ans is None else str(ans)
    43	    if kind == "yesno" or (kind is None and ans.lower() in ("yes", "no")):
    44	        return region.lower().lstrip("*\"' ").startswith(ans.lower())
    45	    if kind == "number" or (kind is None and _num(ans)):
    46	        got = last_number(region.replace("**", ""))
    47	        if got is None:
    48	            return False
    49	        if ans in ("0", "-0"):
    50	            return got in ("0", "-0")
    51	        return got.lstrip("0") == ans.lstrip("0")
    52	    options = (prob or {}).get("options") or [ans]
    53	    hits = [(m.start(), o) for o in options for m in re.finditer(re.escape(o.lower()), region.lower())]
    54	    return bool(hits) and max(hits)[1] == ans
    55	
    56	
    57	def _interval(value):
    58	    if isinstance(value, dict) and "k" in value and "n" in value:
    59	        return value
    60	    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("correct"), dict) and "k" in value["correct"]:
    61	        return value["correct"]
    62	    return None
    63	
    64	
    65	def loop_counts(doc):
    66	    clock = doc.get("clock") or doc
    67	    summary = clock["summary"]
    68	    cells = {}
    69	    for key, value in summary.items():
    70	        if str(key).startswith("_"):
    71	            continue
    72	        got = _interval(value)
    73	        if got is not None:
    74	            cells[key] = got
    75	    summed = interval_record(sum(v["k"] for v in cells.values()), sum(v["n"] for v in cells.values()))
    76	    records = clock.get("records") or []
    77	    from_flag = interval_record(sum(bool(r.get("correct")) for r in records), len(records)) if records else None
    78	    return {"all": _interval(summary.get("_all")), "cells": summed, "records_flag": from_flag,
    79	            "n_cells": len(cells), "n_records": len(records), "records": records}
    80	
    81	
    82	def recount(path, records):
    83	    """Second scorer: the saved answer region, the problem's full answer, reason_loop's extractors."""
    84	    sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "reason"))
    85	    import reason_loop as RL
    86	    probs, src = _problems(path)
    87	    if not probs or len(probs) != len(records):
    88	        return None
    89	    k = sum(rescore_pair(r, p, RL.last_number, RL.answer_region) for r, p in zip(records, probs))
    90	    return {"source": src, **interval_record(k, len(records))}
    91	
    92	
    93	def main(argv):
    94	    paths = argv or []
    95	    if not paths:
    96	        raise SystemExit("pass result json paths")
    97	    out = []
    98	    for path in paths:
    99	        doc = json.loads(pathlib.Path(path).read_text())
   100	        counts = loop_counts(doc)
   101	        second = recount(path, counts.pop("records"))
   102	        counts["path"] = path
   103	        counts["records_rescore"] = second
   104	        keys = [counts["all"]["k"], counts["cells"]["k"], counts["records_flag"]["k"]]
   105	        if second is not None:
   106	            keys.append(second["k"])
   107	        counts["match"] = len(set(keys)) == 1
   108	        out.append(counts)
   109	    print(json.dumps(out, indent=1))
   110	    if not all(c["match"] for c in out):
   111	        raise SystemExit(1)
   112	
   113	
   114	if __name__ == "__main__":
   115	    main(sys.argv[1:])

  1336	
  1337	`r4_report.py` prints k, n and Wilson intervals from the result JSON. It checks the pooled k against the sum of the cells, against the records' `correct` flags, and against a second scorer (the saved answer region, the problem's full answer, `last_number` / `answer_region`). A report that does not match is not entered in this file.

  1625	2. Seed 777, R1 clock, the HELD cells, n = 16: r4's rate is at least reason-s1's rate on those cells plus 0.05. reason-s1's rate is the sum of the r1a and r1b records, read by `r4_report.py`.

  1627	4. Seed 777, R2 clock, the PHRASE cells, n = 8: r4 is no more than 0.05 below reason-s1.

  1629	6. Pinned and Mealy accuracy in the counted train record are 1.0 at the last logged step.
  1630	
  1631	## What could be wrong
  1632	
  1633	1. A container running yesterday's sources. The image env `R4_SOURCES_SHA256` changes when the sources change, the worker overwrites `/root/pb` with the shipped files and refuses on a hash mismatch, and a job is spawned only from a clean commit. Ruled out for a job when its log shows the matching hash and the new error text.
  1634	2. A merged adapter left in `/tmp` by a warm container. The worker deletes `/tmp/pb_*merged*` and `reason_loop` merges to `/tmp/pb_reason_merged_{PB_JOB_ID}`. Ruled out when the job log shows a merge into that path.
  1635	3. A typed or unpaired count. `r4_report.py` is the only way a count enters this file, and the second scorer has to match. Comparisons use the same cells and the same seed. Ruled out per table when the script prints `"match": true`.
  1636	
  1637	Evidence. The counted jobs exited without a source-hash error. `research/r4/results/r4-r1a/job.log.json` records the merge path `/tmp/pb_reason_merged_r4-r1a`. The clock, no-clock, compile, family, G1, and addition blocks above all have `"match": true`.
    50	LT_ID = 27            # "<": in the model's words it opens only a program (finals are written by the clock)

    53	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ problems
    54	
    55	def parse_cells(spec, n_default):
    56	    cells = []
    57	    for part in [p for p in spec.split(";") if p.strip()]:
    58	        bits = part.strip().split(":")
    59	        t, split, lengths = bits[0], bits[1], [int(x) for x in bits[2].split(",")]
    60	        n = int(bits[3]) if len(bits) > 3 else n_default
    61	        cells.append((t, split, lengths, n))
    62	    return cells
    63	
    64	
    65	def make_problems(tok, cells, seed, mixed_digits=100):
    66	    """The same problems for every model: one seeded stream per cell, in cell order."""
    67	    probs = []
    68	    for ci, (t, split, lengths, n) in enumerate(cells):
    69	        for L in lengths:
    70	            rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|{t}|{split}|{L}")
    71	            for j in range(n):
    72	                kw = {"digits": mixed_digits} if t in D.MIXED_TEMPLATES else {}
    73	                ex = D.example(tok, rng, t, L, split, **kw)
    74	                probs.append({"cell": f"{t}|{split}|L{L}", "template": t, "split": split, "L": L,
    75	                              "question": ex["question"], "answer": ex["answer"], "answer_kind": ex["answer_kind"],
    76	                              "options": ex.get("options"), "ref_calls": ex["ref_calls"],
    77	                              "truth_values": ex["truth_values"], "phrasing": ex["phrasing"],
    78	                              "heldout_phrasing": ex.get("heldout_phrasing", False),
    79	                              "digits": ex.get("digits")})
    80	    return probs
    81	
    82	
    83	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ answers
    84	
    85	def last_number(text):
    86	    runs = re.findall(r"-?\d(?:[\d,   ]*\d)?", text.replace("−", "-"))
    87	    if not runs:
    88	        return None
    89	    r = runs[-1]
    90	    neg = r.startswith("-")
    91	    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", r)
    92	    return ("-" if neg else "") + digits
    93	
    94	
    95	def answer_region(text):
    96	    """The text after the last final (ours) or after </think> (thinking models); an `Answer:` line if present."""
    97	    tail = text.split("</final>")[-1].split("</think>")[-1]
    98	    lines = re.findall(r"Answer\s*[:：]\s*\**\s*(.+)", tail)
    99	    return (lines[-1] if lines else tail).strip(), bool(lines)
   100	
   101	
   102	def correct(prob, text):
   103	    region, _ = answer_region(text)
   104	    kind, ans = prob["answer_kind"], prob["answer"]
   105	    if kind == "number":
   106	        got = last_number(region.replace("**", ""))
   107	        return got is not None and got.lstrip("0") == ans.lstrip("0") if ans not in ("0", "-0") else got in ("0",)
   108	    if kind == "yesno":
   109	        low = region.lower().lstrip("*\"' ")
   110	        return low.startswith(ans.lower())
   111	    hits = [(m.start(), o) for o in (prob.get("options") or [ans])
   112	            for m in re.finditer(re.escape(o.lower()), region.lower())]
   113	    return bool(hits) and max(hits)[1] == ans
   114	
   115	
   116	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the executor (pinned, certified)
   117	
   118	class Executor:
   119	    def __init__(self, hf, tok, check_first):
   120	        self.hf, self.tok, self.check_first = hf, tok, check_first
   121	        self.certs = {}
   122	
   123	    def _cert(self, reg):
   124	        key = (reg["kind"], tuple(reg["pi"]))
   125	        if key not in self.certs:
   126	            import pb_pin
   127	            t0 = time.time()
   128	            if reg["kind"] == "mealy":
   129	                cert, past, logits = A.certify_mealy(self.hf, reg["pi"], reg["rows"], keep_logits=True)
   130	            else:
   131	                cert, past, logits = pb_pin.certify(self.hf, reg["pi"], reg["rows"], keep_logits=True)
   132	            self.certs[key] = {"cert": cert, "past": past, "logits": logits, "index": len(self.certs),
   133	                               "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "uses": 0}
   134	        c = self.certs[key]
   135	        c["uses"] += 1
   136	        return c
   137	
   138	    def __call__(self, prog, reading, full_real):
   139	        import pb_pin
   140	        import torch
   141	        info = {"registers": [], "ticks": 0, "real": 0, "bitwise_equal": 0, "argmax_differs": 0}
   142	        if prog["mode"] == "tape":
   143	            states, finals = {}, {}
   144	            for r in prog["registers"]:
   145	                c = self._cert(r)
   146	                seq, real, same, bad = pb_pin.run_lookup(self.hf, c["past"], len(r["pi"]), r["start"], reading["tape"],
   147	                                                         c["cert"], c["logits"], check_first=self.check_first,
   148	                                                         check_all=full_real)
   149	                states[r["name"]], finals[r["name"]] = seq, (seq[-1] if seq else r["start"])
   150	                info["registers"].append({"name": r["name"], "pi_index": c["index"], "certified": c["cert"]["pass"],
   151	                                          "rows": len(r["rows"]), "rows_wrong": c["cert"]["rows_wrong"],
   152	                                          "min_margin": c["cert"]["min_margin"]})
   153	                info["ticks"] += len(seq)
   154	                info["real"] += real
   155	                info["bitwise_equal"] += same
   156	                info["argmax_differs"] += bad
   157	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   158	            return {"states": states, "finals": finals}, info
   159	        r = prog["registers"][0]
   160	        c = self._cert(r)
   161	        seq, outs, real, same, differ = A.run_mealy(self.hf, c["past"], len(r["pi"]), r["start"], reading["pairs"],
   162	                                                    c["cert"], c["logits"],
   163	                                                    check_first=None if full_real else self.check_first)
   164	        info["registers"].append({"name": r["name"], "pi_index": c["index"], "certified": c["cert"]["pass"],
   165	                                  "rows": len(r["rows"]), "rows_wrong": c["cert"]["rows_wrong"],
   166	                                  "min_margin": c["cert"]["min_margin"]})
   167	        info.update(ticks=len(seq), real=real, bitwise_equal=same, argmax_differs=differ)
   168	        return {"states": {r["name"]: seq}, "outputs": outs, "finals": {r["name"]: seq[-1]}}, info
   169	
   170	
   171	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ scoring a call against its reference
   172	
   173	def call_vs_ref(tok, prog, reading, ref):
   174	    """compile: the tables (tape) or the table's operation (numbers) equal the reference's; reading: the clock's tape
   175	    or numbers equal the reference's."""
   176	    if ref is None or prog is None or prog.get("error"):
   177	        return None, None
   178	    if ref["mode"] != prog["mode"]:
   179	        return False, False
   180	    if ref["mode"] == "tape":
   181	        want = [{"start": pb_lit.name_id(tok, t["start"]),
   182	                 "rows": {(pb_lit.name_id(tok, s), pb_lit.name_id(tok, e)): pb_lit.name_id(tok, x)
   183	                          for s, e, x in t["rows"]}} for t in ref["tables"]]
   184	        got = [{"start": r["start"], "rows": {(s, e): t for s, e, t in r["rows"]}} for r in prog["registers"]]
   185	        tape = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, e) for e in ref["tape"]]
   186	        return got == want, reading is not None and reading.get("tape") == tape
   187	    reg = prog["registers"][0]
   188	    table = {row[:3]: row[3:] for row in reg["rows"]}
   189	    d = [A.digit_id(tok, x) for x in range(10)]
   190	    want = {(d[int(r[0])], d[int(r[1])], d[int(r[2])]): (d[int(r[3])], d[int(r[4])]) for r in R.table_rows(ref["op"])}
   191	    comp = table == want and len(reg["rows"]) == 200 and reg["start"] == d[0]
   192	    return comp, reading is not None and reading.get("numbers") == list(ref["numbers"])
   193	
   194	
   195	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the loop
   196	
   197	def run_loop(llm, tok, probs, mode, executor, args, log=print, progress_path=None):
   198	    from vllm import SamplingParams, TokensPrompt
   199	    enc = lambda s: R.enc(tok, s)  # noqa: E731
   200	    opener = enc("\n<run>\n")
   201	    st = [{"prompt": enc(R.chat_prompt(p["question"])), "resp": [], "results": [], "calls": [], "done": False,
   202	           "finish": None, "gen_words": 0, "gen_calls": 0, "segments": []} for p in probs]
   203	    full_real_seen = {}
   204	    t0 = time.time()
   205	    for rnd in range(R.HARD_STOP_CALLS + 1):
   206	        active = [i for i, s in enumerate(st) if not s["done"]]
   207	        if not active:
   208	            break
   209	        prompts, params, keep = [], [], []
   210	        for i in active:
   211	            s = st[i]
   212	            room = CONTEXT - len(s["prompt"]) - len(s["resp"]) - 8
   213	            budget = min(args.max_new, room)
   214	            if budget < 16:
   215	                s["done"], s["finish"] = True, "context"
   216	                continue
   217	            prompts.append(TokensPrompt(prompt_token_ids=s["prompt"] + s["resp"]))
   218	            params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=budget, stop=["<exec/>"],
   219	                                         include_stop_str_in_output=True))
   220	            keep.append(i)
   221	        if not keep:
   222	            break
   223	        outs = llm.generate(prompts, params, use_tqdm=False)
   224	        n_exec = 0
   225	        for i, o in zip(keep, outs):
   226	            s, p = st[i], probs[i]
   227	            out = o.outputs[0]
   228	            ids = [t for t in out.token_ids if t != args.im_end]
   229	            hit = out.stop_reason == "<exec/>" or tok.decode(ids).rstrip().endswith("<exec/>")
   230	            if not hit:
   231	                s["resp"] += ids
   232	                s["gen_words"] += len(ids)
   233	                s["segments"].append({"type": "text", "ids": ids})
   234	                s["done"], s["finish"] = True, out.finish_reason
   235	                continue
   236	            start = R.last_call_start(tok, ids)       # this round's ids only: a call needs a new <program> (§12)
   237	            words, call = (ids[:start], ids[start:]) if start is not None else (ids, [])
   238	            s["gen_words"] += len(words)
   239	            s["gen_calls"] += len(call)
   240	            if words:
   241	                s["segments"].append({"type": "text", "ids": words})
   242	            if len(s["calls"]) >= R.HARD_STOP_CALLS:
   243	                # the 12th <exec/> ends the response; nothing is appended (§12, as reason_core.clock_step)
   244	                s["resp"] += ids
   245	                s["segments"].append({"type": "call", "ids": call, "hard_stop": True})
   246	                s["done"], s["finish"] = True, "hard stop"
   247	                continue
   248	            s["resp"] += ids
   249	            n_exec += 1
   250	            if mode == "plain":
   251	                s["resp"] += opener
   252	                s["segments"].append({"type": "call", "ids": call, "plain": True})
   253	                s["calls"].append({"plain": True})
   254	                continue
   255	            text = tok.decode(s["resp"])
   256	            rep = R.repeat_refusal(text, s["results"]) if call else None
   257	            if not call:
   258	                prog = {"mode": None, "error": "no program", "registers": []}
   259	                reading = {"error": "no program", "spans": [], "how": []}
   260	            elif rep:
   261	                prog = {"mode": None, "error": rep, "registers": [], "anchors": None, "numbers": {}}
   262	                reading = {"error": rep, "spans": [], "how": []}
   263	            else:
   264	                prog = R.parse_call(tok, call)
   265	                reading = R.read_call(tok, p["question"], text, prog, s["results"])
   266	            rec = {"mode": prog["mode"], "error": reading["error"], "spans": reading["spans"], "how": reading["how"]}
   267	            if len(s["results"]) >= R.MAX_CALLS:
   268	                rec["error"] = "too many calls"
   269	            if rec["error"]:
   270	                fin = R.error_final_ids(tok, rec["error"])
   271	                value = None
   272	            else:
   273	                full = full_real_seen.get(p["cell"], 0) < args.full_real_per_cell
   274	                run, info = executor(prog, reading, full)
   275	                fin = R.final_for(tok, prog, run)
   276	                value = R.result_value(tok, fin)
   277	                oracle = R.execute_oracle(prog, reading)
   278	                rec.update(info, full_real=full, run_exact=(run["finals"] == oracle["finals"] and
   279	                                                            run.get("outputs") == oracle.get("outputs")),
   280	                           certified=all(x["certified"] for x in info["registers"]),
   281	                           min_margin=min((x["min_margin"] for x in info["registers"]), default=None),
   282	                           events=len(reading.get("tape") or reading.get("pairs") or []))
   283	                if prog["mode"] == "numbers":
   284	                    rec["numbers_len"] = [len(x) for x in reading["numbers"]]
   285	            k_ok = sum(1 for c in s["calls"] if not c.get("error") and not c.get("plain"))
   286	            ref = p["ref_calls"][k_ok] if not rec["error"] and k_ok < len(p["ref_calls"]) else None
   287	            comp, readok = call_vs_ref(tok, prog, reading if not rec["error"] else None, ref)
   288	            rec.update(value=value, compile=comp, reading_exact=readok, call_tokens=len(call),
   289	                       program_text=tok.decode(call))
   290	            s["calls"].append(rec)
   291	            s["segments"].append({"type": "call", "ids": call, "call": len(s["calls"]) - 1})
   292	            s["segments"].append({"type": "final", "ids": fin})
   293	            s["results"].append(value)
   294	            s["resp"] += fin
   295	            if not rec["error"]:
   296	                full_real_seen[p["cell"]] = full_real_seen.get(p["cell"], 0) + (1 if rec.get("full_real") else 0)
   297	        log(json.dumps({"mode": mode, "round": rnd, "active": len(keep), "exec": n_exec,
   298	                        "seconds": round(time.time() - t0)}))
   299	        if progress_path is not None:
   300	            # a job cut off by its time limit keeps every finished problem's score
   301	            atomic_json(progress_path, {"mode": mode, "round": rnd, "problems": [
   302	                {"cell": p["cell"], "done": s["done"], "finish": s["finish"], "n_calls": len(s["calls"]),
   303	                 "tokens": s["gen_words"] + s["gen_calls"],
   304	                 "correct": (correct(p, tok.decode(s["resp"])) and s["finish"] == "stop") if s["done"] else None,
   305	                 "call_values": [c.get("value") for c in s["calls"]][:8],
   306	                 "call_errors": [c.get("error") for c in s["calls"]][:8]} for p, s in zip(probs, st)]})
   307	    return st
   308	
   309	
   310	def p_open_trace(hf, tok, prompt, resp, segments, chunk=2048):
   339	def transcript_json(tok, prob, s, p_open):
   340	    segs, pos = [], 0
   341	    for seg in s["segments"]:
   342	        n = len(seg["ids"])
   343	        entry = {"type": seg["type"], "text": tok.decode(seg["ids"])}
   344	        if seg["type"] == "text" and p_open is not None:
   345	            entry["tokens"] = [tok.decode([t]) for t in seg["ids"]]
   346	            entry["p_open"] = [round(x, 5) for x in p_open[pos:pos + n]]
   347	        if seg["type"] == "call":
   348	            entry["p_open_at_start"] = round(p_open[pos], 5) if p_open is not None and pos < len(p_open) else None
   349	            if "call" in seg:
   350	                c = s["calls"][seg["call"]]
   351	                entry["clock"] = {k: c.get(k) for k in ("mode", "error", "spans", "how", "value", "certified",
   352	                                                        "min_margin", "registers", "ticks", "real", "bitwise_equal",
   353	                                                        "argmax_differs", "run_exact", "compile", "reading_exact",
   354	                                                        "events", "full_real")}
   355	        segs.append(entry)
   356	        pos += n
   357	    return {"cell": prob["cell"], "question": prob["question"], "answer": prob["answer"], "segments": segs,
   358	            "correct": s.get("correct"), "finish": s["finish"]}
   359	
   360	
   361	def summarize(probs, st, mode):
   362	    def rate(xs):
   363	        return interval_record(sum(bool(x) for x in xs), len(xs)) if xs else None
   364	    cells = {}
   365	    for p, s in zip(probs, st):
   366	        cells.setdefault(p["cell"], []).append((p, s))
   367	    out = {}
   368	    for cell, rows in sorted(cells.items()):
   369	        calls = [c for _, s in rows for c in s["calls"] if not c.get("plain")]
   370	        ok = [p_s[1]["correct"] for p_s in rows]
   371	        out[cell] = {
   372	            "correct": rate(ok),
   373	            "made_a_call": rate([bool(s["calls"]) for _, s in rows]),
   374	            "calls_per_problem": round(sum(len(s["calls"]) for _, s in rows) / len(rows), 2),
   375	            "ref_calls_per_problem": round(sum(len(p["ref_calls"]) for p, _ in rows) / len(rows), 2),
   376	            "call_errors": sum(bool(c.get("error")) for c in calls),
   377	            "recovered": sum(s.get("recovered", 0) for _, s in rows),
   378	            "compile": rate([c["compile"] for c in calls if c.get("compile") is not None]),
   379	            "reading": rate([c["reading_exact"] for c in calls if c.get("reading_exact") is not None]),
   380	            "certified": rate([c["certified"] for c in calls if "certified" in c]),
   381	            "run_exact": rate([c["run_exact"] for c in calls if "run_exact" in c]),
   382	            "tokens_words_mean": round(sum(s["gen_words"] for _, s in rows) / len(rows), 1),
   383	            "tokens_calls_mean": round(sum(s["gen_calls"] for _, s in rows) / len(rows), 1),
   384	            "finish": {f: sum(1 for _, s in rows if s["finish"] == f) for f in {s["finish"] for _, s in rows}},
   385	        }
   386	    allc = [c for s in st for c in s["calls"] if not c.get("plain") and "real" in c]
   387	    out["_ticks"] = {"ticks": sum(c["ticks"] for c in allc), "real": sum(c["real"] for c in allc),
   388	                     "real_bitwise_equal_to_row": sum(c["bitwise_equal"] for c in allc),
   389	                     "real_argmax_differs_from_row": sum(c["argmax_differs"] for c in allc),
   390	                     "full_real_runs": sum(bool(c.get("full_real")) for c in allc),
   391	                     "certified_but_run_inexact": sum(bool(c.get("certified")) and not c.get("run_exact")
   392	                                                      for c in allc)}
   393	    out["_all"] = {"correct": rate([s["correct"] for s in st]),
   394	                   "tokens_generated_mean": round(sum(s["gen_words"] + s["gen_calls"] for s in st) / max(1, len(st)), 1)}
   395	    return out
   396

   446	                not d.get("error") for d in s["calls"][k + 1:]))

     2	r4 SFT. reason_train's loop, from reason-s1, with three more mix kinds:

    28	# Registered mix (R4.md). Weights sum to 1. KL kinds are ordinary, general and negarith.
    29	REGISTERED_MIX = (
    30	    "ordinary=0.25,general=0.20,negarith=0.04,reason=0.14,layout=0.06,compile=0.08,repair=0.05,"
    31	    "reason_plain=0.03,arith=0.03,sub=0.02,lit=0.03,gen=0.03,interp=0.02,verify=0.01,family=0.01"
    32	)
    33	
    34	PLANS = (
    35	    "I'll compile the rules into a table and run it.",
    36	    "The list is long, so the program tracks it.",
    37	    "Writing the table the rules describe, then reading the result.",
    38	)

    41	class R4Stream(RT.ReasonStream):
    42	    def __init__(self, tok, args, general, ordinary=(), negarith=()):
    43	        super().__init__(tok, args, general, ordinary, negarith)
    44	        extra = ("layout", "compile", "repair")
    45	        rest = {k: v for k, v in self.rest_mix.items() if k not in extra}
    46	        self.rest_mix = {k: v / sum(rest.values()) for k, v in rest.items()} if rest else {}
    47	        self.repair_items = []
    48	        path = getattr(args, "repair_file", "") or ""
    49	        if path and pathlib.Path(path).is_file():
    50	            import json
    51	            self.repair_items = json.loads(pathlib.Path(path).read_text()).get("items", [])
    52	        self._V = None
    53	
    54	    def _vocab(self):
    55	        if self._V is None:
    56	            import compile_gen as G
    57	            self._V = G.Vocab(self.tok)
    58	        return self._V
    59	
    60	    def _layout(self):
    61	        ex = Q.layout_example(self.tok, self.rng, self.im_end)
    62	        return None if ex is None else ex["_built"]
    63	
    64	    def _compile(self):
    65	        import compile_gen as G
    66	        rng = self.rng
    67	        if rng.random() < 0.22:
    68	            ex, _why = G.number_example(rng, self.tok, D.loguniform_int(rng, 2, 48), "iid", lib_p=0.85)
    69	        else:
    70	            hi = 512 if rng.random() < 0.15 else 128
    71	            ex, _why = G.example(rng, self._vocab(), self.tok, D.loguniform_int(rng, 4, hi), "iid", lib_p=0.75)
    72	        if ex is None:
    73	            return None
    74	        call = G.program_ids(self.tok, ex)
    75	        if any(i is None for i in call):
    76	            return None
    77	        segs = [("text", rng.choice(PLANS) + "\n\n"), ("call", call), ("text", f"Answer: {ex['answer']}")]
    78	        try:
    79	            ids, labels, calls = R.build(self.tok, ex["question"], segs, self.im_end)
    80	        except ValueError:
    81	            return None
    82	        if any(c.get("error") for c in calls):
    83	            return None
    84	        return ids, labels
    85	
    86	    def _repair(self):
    87	        rng = self.rng
    88	        if self.repair_items and rng.random() < 0.7:
    89	            item = self.repair_items[rng.randrange(len(self.repair_items))]
    90	            pair = Q.on_policy_repair(self.tok, item, self.im_end)
    91	            if pair:
    92	                return pair
    93	        return Q.conceptual_repair(self.tok, rng, self.im_end)
    94	
    95	    def _fallback(self):
    96	        ex = D.example(self.tok, self.rng, "shelf_bill", 8, "train", im_end=self.im_end)
    97	        ids, labels, _ = R.build(self.tok, ex["question"], D.segments_ids(self.tok, ex), self.im_end)
    98	        return ids, labels
    99	
   100	    def _extra(self, kind):
   101	        fn = {"layout": self._layout, "compile": self._compile, "repair": self._repair}.get(kind)
   102	        if fn is None:
   103	            return None
   104	        for _ in range(8):
   105	            pair = fn()
   106	            if pair:
   107	                return pair
   108	        return self._fallback()
   109	
   110	
   111	def main():
   112	    RT.STREAM_CLS = R4Stream
   113	    RT.main()

   117	    main()
     1	"""

     3

    31	def freeze(o):
    32	    if isinstance(o, tuple):
    33	        return {"__tuple__": [freeze(x) for x in o]}
    34	    if isinstance(o, list):
    35	        return [freeze(x) for x in o]
    36	    if isinstance(o, dict):
    37	        return {str(k): freeze(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    38	    return o
    39	
    40	
    41	def thaw(o):
    42	    if isinstance(o, list):
    43	        return [thaw(x) for x in o]
    44	    if isinstance(o, dict):
    45	        if set(o) == {"__tuple__"}:
    46	            return tuple(thaw(x) for x in o["__tuple__"])
    47	        return {k: thaw(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    48	    return o
    49	
    50	
    51	def _call(op, first, second):
    52	    return {"kind": "numbers", "op": op, "first": first, "second": second}
    53	
    54	
    55	def _run(tok, question, segs, im_end, values):
    56	    ids, labels, calls = R.build(tok, question, D.segments_ids(tok, {"segments": segs}), im_end)
    57	    good = [c for c in calls if not c["error"]]
    58	    if len(good) != len(values):
    59	        raise AssertionError((len(good), len(values), [c.get("error") for c in calls]))
    60	    for c, v in zip(good, values):
    61	        if str(c["value"]) != str(v):
    62	            raise AssertionError((c["value"], v, [c.get("error") for c in calls]))
    63	    return ids, labels, calls

    66	def layout_example(tok, rng, im_end=None):
    67	    """One grammar layout. Returns the example record, or None."""
    68	    im_end = im_end if im_end is not None else tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
    69	    nums = [D.random_number(rng, D.loguniform_int(rng, 1, 20)) for _ in range(4)]
    70	    iv = [int(n) for n in nums]
    71	    style = rng.randrange(4)
    72	    if style == 0:
    73	        q = (f"In year one Ada sold {nums[0]} units and Bo sold {nums[1]} units; "
    74	             f"in year two Ada sold {nums[2]} units and Bo sold {nums[3]} units. "
    75	             f"By how much did the larger two-year total win?")
    76	        a, b = iv[0] + iv[2], iv[1] + iv[3]
    77	        first, second = (1, 2) if a >= b else (2, 1)
    78	        calls = [_call("add", ("number", 1), ("number", 3)),
    79	                 _call("add", ("number", 2), ("number", 4)),
    80	                 _call("sub", ("result", first), ("result", second))]
    81	        values = [str(a), str(b), str(abs(a - b))]
    82	        answer = abs(a - b)
    83	        words = ["Ada's years, then Bo's, then the gap.\n\n", "That is Ada's total.\n\n",
    84	                 "That is Bo's total.\n\n", f"Answer: {answer}"]
    85	    elif style == 1:
    86	        q = (f"The readings are {nums[0]} (first), {nums[1]} (second), {nums[2]} (third) and "
    87	             f"{nums[3]} (fourth). What do you get by adding the first to the fourth, then the second, "
    88	             f"then the third, then adding that to the first sum?")
    89	        s1 = iv[0] + iv[3]
    90	        s2 = s1 + iv[1]
    91	        s3 = s2 + iv[2]
    92	        s4 = s3 + s1
    93	        calls = [_call("add", ("number", 1), ("number", 4)),
    94	                 _call("add", ("result", 1), ("number", 2)),
    95	                 _call("add", ("result", 2), ("number", 3)),
    96	                 _call("add", ("result", 3), ("result", 1))]
    97	        values = [str(s1), str(s2), str(s3), str(s4)]
    98	        answer = s4
    99	        words = ["Four additions, and the last one cites result 1 and result 3.\n\n",
   100	                 "First plus fourth.\n\n", "Plus the second.\n\n", "Plus the third.\n\n",
   101	                 f"Answer: {answer}"]
   102	    elif style == 2:
   103	        q = f"Cargo of kg {nums[0]}, then kg {nums[1]}, then kg {nums[2]}. What is the total weight in kg?"
   104	        s1, s2 = iv[0] + iv[1], iv[0] + iv[1] + iv[2]
   105	        calls = [_call("add", ("number", 1), ("number", 2)),
   106	                 _call("add", ("result", 1), ("number", 3))]
   107	        values = [str(s1), str(s2)]
   108	        answer = s2
   109	        words = ["Adding the three weights.\n\n", "The first two.\n\n", f"Answer: {answer}"]
   110	    else:
   111	        q = f"Please add {nums[0]} (the deposit) and {nums[1]} (the bonus). What is the total?"
   112	        spans = A.number_spans(q)
   113	        spec = D.number_spec("add", q, [("span", spans[0]), ("span", spans[1])])
   114	        if spec is None:
   115	            calls = [_call("add", ("number", 1), ("number", 2))]
   116	        else:
   117	            calls = [spec]
   118	        answer = iv[0] + iv[1]
   119	        values = [str(answer)]
   120	        words = ["The two amounts are in parentheses, so I'll quote them.\n\n", f"Answer: {answer}"]
   121	    segs = [("text", words[0])]
   122	    for i, spec in enumerate(calls):
   123	        segs.append(("call", spec))
   124	        segs.append(("text", words[i + 1]))
   125	    try:
   126	        ids, labels, calls_out = _run(tok, q, segs, im_end, values)
   127	    except (AssertionError, ValueError):
   128	        return None
   129	    return {"question": q, "segments": segs, "answer": str(answer), "truth_values": values,
   130	            "template": "layout", "style": style, "_built": (ids, labels), "calls": len(calls_out)}
   131	
   132	
   133	def _retarget(lit, new_events):
   134	    """Rewrite each register as a total table over new_events (names)."""
   135	    for reg in lit["registers"]:
   136	        states, old = [], []
   137	        for s, e, _t in reg["rows"]:
   138	            if s not in states:
   139	                states.append(s)
   140	            if e not in old:
   141	                old.append(e)
   142	        rows, seen = [], set()
   143	        for s in states:
   144	            for e in new_events:
   145	                dest = s
   146	                for s2, e2, t in reg["rows"]:
   147	                    if s2 == s and e2 == (old[new_events.index(e) % len(old)] if old else e):
   148	                        dest = t
   149	                        break
   150	                if (s, e) not in seen:
   151	                    rows.append((s, e, dest))
   152	                    seen.add((s, e))
   153	        reg["rows"] = rows
   154	    return lit
   155	
   156	
   157	def _insert(tok, ex, bad_ids, repair, im_end):
   158	    segs = ex["segments"]
   159	    k = next(i for i, (kind, _) in enumerate(segs) if kind == "call")
   160	    pre = D.segments_ids(tok, {"segments": segs[:k]})
   161	    post = D.segments_ids(tok, {"segments": segs[k:]})
   162	    try:
   163	        ids, labels, calls = R.build(tok, ex["question"], pre + [("bad", list(bad_ids)), ("text", repair)] + post,
   164	                                     im_end)
   165	    except (ValueError, AssertionError):
   166	        return None
   167	    good = [c for c in calls if not c["error"]]
   168	    if not any(c["error"] for c in calls):
   169	        return None
   170	    vals = ex.get("truth_values") or []
   171	    if len(good) != len(vals):
   172	        return None
   173	    for c, v in zip(good, vals):
   174	        if v is not None and str(c["value"]) != str(v):
   175	            return None
   176	    return ids, labels, calls
   177	
   178	
   179	def conceptual_repair(tok, rng, im_end, kind=None):
   180	    """One synthetic fault of the kind REASON measured, paired with the reference call. None if this draw misses."""
   181	    kind = kind or rng.choice(["alphabet", "moore", "bare"])
   182	    if kind == "alphabet":
   183	        ex = D.example(tok, rng, "shelf_bill", D.loguniform_int(rng, 4, 24), "train", im_end=im_end)
   184	        spec = next(v for k, v in ex["segments"] if k == "call")
   185	        lit = json.loads(json.dumps(spec["lit"]))
   186	        _retarget(lit, ["0", "1"])
   187	        bad = R.tape_program_ids(tok, lit)
   188	        repair = ("The table events are 0, 1 but the log is not. I'll write a row for each log word.\n\n")
   189	        built = _insert(tok, ex, bad, repair, im_end)
   190	        if built and "table events" in (built[2][0]["error"] or ""):
   191	            return built[0], built[1]
   192	        return None
   193	    if kind == "moore":
   194	        ex = D.sub_example(tok, rng, "train", max_L=8, im_end=im_end)
   195	        call_ids = next(val for k, val in D.segments_ids(tok, ex) if k == "call")
   196	        text = tok.decode(call_ids)
   197	        bad_text = text.replace(" (output d):", ":").replace(" (output s):", ":")
   198	        if bad_text == text:
   199	            return None
   200	        repair = "Rows have 5 tokens, so the header needs (output …). Rewriting the register line.\n\n"
   201	        built = _insert(tok, ex, R.enc(tok, bad_text), repair, im_end)
   202	        if built and "rows have 5 tokens" in (built[2][0]["error"] or ""):
   203	            return built[0], built[1]
   204	        return None
   205	    ex = D.example(tok, rng, rng.choice(["deposit_chain", "sales_compare"]), D.loguniform_int(rng, 2, 12), "train",
   206	                   im_end=im_end)
   207	    call_ids = next(val for k, val in D.segments_ids(tok, ex) if k == "call")
   208	    text = tok.decode(call_ids)
   209	    bad_text, n = re.subn(r"(?m)^(second|first): .*$", r"\1: result", text, count=1)
   210	    if not n:
   211	        return None
   212	    repair = "A citation is written `result k` with the number. Rewriting that line.\n\n"
   213	    built = _insert(tok, ex, R.enc(tok, bad_text), repair, im_end)
   214	    if built and "write result" in (built[2][0]["error"] or ""):
   215	        return built[0], built[1]
   216	    return None
   217	
   218	
   219	def on_policy_repair(tok, item, im_end):
   220	    """Dagger item: the model's faulty call, masked, in the reference transcript, then the reference call."""
   221	    ex = thaw(item["example"])
   222	    repair = item.get("repair") or "That call failed. I'll write the program the question asks for.\n\n"
   223	    built = _insert(tok, ex, item["faulty_ids"], repair, im_end)
   224	    if built is None:
   225	        return None
   226	    return built[0], built[1]
   227	
   228	
   229	def selftest(tok, n=8, seed=0):
   230	    rng = random.Random(seed)
   231	    im_end = tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   232	    layouts = 0
   233	    for _ in range(n * 4):
   234	        if layout_example(tok, rng, im_end):
   235	            layouts += 1
   236	        if layouts >= n:
   237	            break
   238	    found = {}
   239	    for kind in ("alphabet", "moore", "bare"):
   240	        for i in range(25):
   241	            if conceptual_repair(tok, random.Random(1000 + i), im_end, kind=kind):
   242	                found[kind] = found.get(kind, 0) + 1
   243	                break
   244	    assert layouts >= n, layouts
   245	    assert set(found) == {"alphabet", "moore", "bare"}, found
   246	    ex = layout_example(tok, rng, im_end)
   247	    assert thaw(freeze(ex["segments"]))[0][0] == "text"
   248	    # a held-out REASON template still round-trips under the new clock
   249	    ex2 = D.example(tok, rng, "four_total", 8, "test", im_end=im_end)
   250	    D.verify(tok, ex2, im_end)
   251	    ex3 = D.example(tok, rng, "coin_add_or_sub", 16, "test", im_end=im_end)
   252	    D.verify(tok, ex3, im_end)
   253	    return {"layouts": layouts, "repairs": found}
   254	
   255	
   256	if __name__ == "__main__":
   257	    if "--selftest" in sys.argv:
   258	        print(json.dumps(selftest(R.load_tokenizer())))
     1	"""
     2	On-policy repairs from the current adapter (DAgger). Training prompts only: REASON training templates and r4
     3	layouts. Each failing call is kept only if, dropped into the reference transcript, the clock rejects it and the
     4	reference calls still match Python. The faulty call is what training masks; the reference call is the target.
     5	
     6	Generation uses vLLM. The clock here is the Python oracle (the same one training replays). No second copy of the
     7	weights is loaded.
     8	
     9	    python r4_dagger.py --adapter /reasonvol/reason-s1/adapter_step500 --n 4 --out /vol/dagger
    10	"""

    26	import r4_data as Q  # noqa: E402
    27	import reason_core as R  # noqa: E402
    28	import reason_data as D  # noqa: E402
    29	import reason_loop as RL  # noqa: E402
    30	from pb_common import atomic_json, host_record, stamp  # noqa: E402

    32	TRAIN_LEN = {
    33	    "shelf_bill": (256, 1024), "robot_charger": (256, 1024), "coin_bet": (128, 512), "cups_count": (64, 256),
    34	    "gate_heading": (128, 512), "truth_chain": (64, 256), "dance_swaps": (64, 256),
    35	    "sales_compare": (20, 80), "deposit_chain": (20, 80), "parity_pick_sum": (64, 256),
    36	}
    37	
    38	
    39	def oracle_exec(prog, reading, full):
    40	    run = R.execute_oracle(prog, reading)
    41	    # min_margin is a number: reason_loop takes min() over it, and None is not ordered
    42	    info = {"registers": [{"certified": True, "min_margin": 0.0, "name": r["name"]} for r in prog["registers"]],
    43	            "ticks": 0, "real": 0, "bitwise_equal": 0, "argmax_differs": 0}
    44	    return run, info
    45	
    46	
    47	def prompts(tok, n, seed, im_end, limit=0):
    48	    """Training prompts and the reference examples they come from. Held-out templates are not drawn.
    49	    Layouts come first. limit stops construction once that many examples exist."""
    50	    rng = random.Random(seed)
    51	    examples = []
    52	
    53	    def add(ex):
    54	        if ex is not None:
    55	            examples.append(ex)
    56	        return limit and len(examples) >= limit
    57	
    58	    # layouts first: the adapter has never seen them, so a short smoke can reach a failing call
    59	    for _ in range(n * 6):
    60	        if add(Q.layout_example(tok, rng, im_end)):
    61	            break
    62	    if not (limit and len(examples) >= limit):
    63	        for template, lengths in TRAIN_LEN.items():
    64	            stop = False
    65	            for L in lengths:
    66	                for _ in range(n):
    67	                    kw = {}
    68	                    if template == "parity_pick_sum":
    69	                        kw["digits"] = 12
    70	                    if add(D.example(tok, rng, template, L, "train", im_end=im_end, **kw)):
    71	                        stop = True
    72	                        break
    73	                if stop:
    74	                    break
    75	            if stop:
    76	                break
    77	    probs = []
    78	    for i, ex in enumerate(examples):
    79	        probs.append({"cell": f"train|{ex.get('template', 'layout')}|{i}", "template": ex.get("template", "layout"),
    80	                      "split": "train", "L": ex.get("L", 0), "question": ex["question"], "answer": str(ex["answer"]),
    81	                      "answer_kind": ex.get("answer_kind", "number"), "options": None,
    82	                      "ref_calls": ex.get("ref_calls") or []})
    83	    return examples, probs
    84	
    85	
    86	def repair_sentence(err):
    87	    return f"The clock said {err.split('(')[0].strip().rstrip('.')}. I'll write a different call.\n\n"
    88	
    89	
    90	def collect(tok, examples, states, im_end):
    91	    items, skipped = [], 0
    92	    for ex, s in zip(examples, states):
    93	        for seg in s["segments"]:
    94	            if seg.get("type") != "call" or "call" not in seg:
    95	                continue
    96	            c = s["calls"][seg["call"]]
    97	            err = c.get("error")
    98	            if not err or err in ("too many calls", "hard stop", "no program") or str(err).startswith("identical to call"):
    99	                continue
   100	            payload = {k: v for k, v in ex.items() if not str(k).startswith("_")}
   101	            item = {"example": Q.freeze(payload), "faulty_ids": list(seg["ids"]), "error": err,
   102	                    "repair": repair_sentence(err), "template": ex.get("template", "layout")}
   103	            if Q.on_policy_repair(tok, item, im_end):
   104	                items.append(item)
   105	            else:
   106	                skipped += 1
   107	            if sum(1 for it in items if it["template"] == item["template"]) > 40:
   108	                break
   109	    return items, skipped
   110	
   111

   113	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()

   125	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   126	    tok = R.load_tokenizer(a.model)
   127	    im_end = tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   128	    t0 = time.time()
   129	    need = (a.prompt_offset + a.max_prompts) if a.max_prompts else 0
   130	    examples, probs = prompts(tok, a.n, a.seed, im_end, limit=need)
   131	    if a.prompt_offset:
   132	        examples, probs = examples[a.prompt_offset:], probs[a.prompt_offset:]
   133	    if a.max_prompts:
   134	        examples, probs = examples[:a.max_prompts], probs[:a.max_prompts]
   135	    print(json.dumps({"prompts": len(probs)}), flush=True)
   136	    from vllm import LLM

   142	
   143	    loop_args = argparse.Namespace(max_new=a.max_new, im_end=im_end, full_real_per_cell=0, check_first=0)
   144	    states = RL.run_loop(llm, tok, probs, "clock", oracle_exec, loop_args, progress_path=out / "dagger_progress.json")
   145	    atomic_json(out / "prompt_view.json", {"rows": [
   146	        {"cell": p["cell"], "finish": s["finish"], "n_calls": len(s["calls"]),
   147	         "errors": [c.get("error") for c in s["calls"]][:8],
   148	         "tail": tok.decode(s["resp"])[-700:]} for p, s in zip(probs, states)]})
   149	    items, skipped = collect(tok, examples, states, im_end)
   150	    atomic_json(out / "repairs.json", {"n_prompts": len(probs), "n_items": len(items), "skipped": skipped,
   151	                                       "seed": a.seed, "adapter": a.adapter, "at": stamp(), "items": items})
   152	    print(json.dumps({"items": len(items), "skipped": skipped, "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1),
   153	                      "host": host_record()}), flush=True)
   154

   157	    main()

     1	"""
     2	REASON RL: GRPO with a verifiable final-answer reward on multi-call transcripts (PROMPTBOX's pb_rl, extended to the
     3	REASON loop). Run only if SFT clearly works (REASON.md).
     4	
     5	Prompts: composite problems from the training templates and training phrasings (lengths up to --max_L events and
     6	--max_digits digits), and --ordinary_frac GSM8K-train / ARC-train questions scored by their own answers.
     7	Rollout: the REASON loop. The model writes until `<exec/>` or `<|im_end|>`; at `<exec/>` the harness runs the clock
     8	with the Python oracle (reason_core.clock_step: the clock's reading of the call, then the written tables run in
     9	Python, equal to the pinned run whenever the certificate passes, which G1 bounds), appends the final line or the error
    10	line, and generation resumes; at most 8 calls. Harness tokens are inputs, never targets.
    11	Reward: r4_reward.score (correct and finished, reference-row fraction, identical-call penalty, length, recovery).
    12	Executor protection, every update: 16 pinned 2-token tables and 16 Mealy tables (as SFT); --sft_weight × the
    13	cross-entropy of reference composite transcripts; --keep_weight × the base model's own replies (self-distillation).

    43	def sample_prompt(rng, tok, ordinary, args, im_end):
    44	    if ordinary and rng.random() < args.ordinary_frac:
    45	        item = ordinary[rng.randrange(len(ordinary))]
    46	        return {"source": "ordinary", "question": item["question"], "item": item}
    47	    t = rng.choice(D.TRAIN_TEMPLATES)

    52	    else:

   130	    def weighted_nll(hs, ts, ws):
   131	        lp = -torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(core.lm_head(hs).float(), ts, reduction="none")
   132	        return -(ws * lp).sum()
   133	
   134	    def forward_logps(seqs, weights):
   135	        seqs = sorted(zip(seqs, weights), key=lambda s: len(s[0][0]))
   136	        total, groups, batch, cur_max = 0.0, [], [], 0
   137	        for s in seqs:
   138	            L = len(s[0][0])
   139	            if batch and max(cur_max, L) * (len(batch) + 1) > args.micro_tokens:
   140	                groups.append(batch)
   141	                batch, cur_max = [], 0
   142	            batch.append(s)
   143	            cur_max = max(cur_max, L)
   144	        if batch:
   145	            groups.append(batch)
   146	        for g in groups:
   147	            L = max(len(ids) for (ids, _), _ in g)
   148	            ids_t = torch.full((len(g), L), im_end, dtype=torch.long)
   149	            lab_t = torch.full((len(g), L), -100, dtype=torch.long)
   150	            w_t = torch.zeros((len(g), L))
   151	            for i, ((ids, lab), w) in enumerate(g):
   152	                ids_t[i, :len(ids)] = torch.tensor(ids)
   153	                lab_t[i, :len(lab)] = torch.tensor(lab)
   154	                w_t[i, :len(lab)] = w
   155	            ids_t, lab_t, w_t = ids_t.cuda(), lab_t.cuda(), w_t.cuda()
   156	            hidden = core.model(input_ids=ids_t).last_hidden_state[:, :-1]
   157	            tgt, w = lab_t[:, 1:], w_t[:, 1:]
   158	            mask = tgt != -100
   159	            h, t, ww = hidden[mask], tgt[mask], w[mask]
   160	            loss = 0.0
   161	            for i in range(0, h.shape[0], 2048):
   162	                loss = loss + checkpoint(weighted_nll, h[i:i + 2048], t[i:i + 2048], ww[i:i + 2048],
   163	                                         use_reentrant=False)
   164	            loss.backward()
   165	            total += float(loss.detach())
   166	            del hidden, loss
   167	        return total
   168	
   169	    def packed(batch_fn, exs, micro, n_labels):
   170	        tot = 0.0
   171	        for k in range(0, len(exs), micro):
   172	            ids, pos, allow, r, c, lab = batch_fn(exs[k:k + micro], im_end)
   173	            hidden = core.model(input_ids=ids.cuda(), position_ids=pos.cuda(), attention_mask=allow.cuda()).last_hidden_state
   174	            loss, _ = chunked_ce(core.lm_head, hidden[r.cuda(), c.cuda()], lab.cuda())
   175	            (loss / n_labels).backward()
   176	            tot += loss.item()
   177	            del hidden, loss
   178	        return tot / max(1, n_labels)
