   185	        roll = []
   186	        for p in prompts:
   187	            pid = R.enc(tok, R.chat_prompt(p["question"]))
   188	            for _ in range(args.group):
   189	                roll.append({"p": p, "pid": pid, "resp": [], "lab": [], "results": [], "done": False, "round_start": 0,
   190	                             "finished": False, "calls": 0, "errors": 0, "gen": 0, "identical": 0,
   191	                             "saw_error": False, "recovered": False, "written": []})
   192	        g0 = time.time()
   193	        for rnd in range(R.HARD_STOP_CALLS + 1):
   194	            active = [x for x in roll if not x["done"]]
   195	            if not active:
   196	                break
   197	            sp = [SamplingParams(temperature=args.temperature, top_p=1.0,
   198	                                 max_tokens=max(1, min(args.max_new, args.max_total - x["gen"],
   199	                                                       RL.CONTEXT - len(x["pid"]) - len(x["resp"]) - 8)),
   200	                                 stop=["<exec/>"], stop_token_ids=[im_end], include_stop_str_in_output=True,
   201	                                 seed=args.seed * 100003 + it * 1009 + rnd * 101 + k) for k, x in enumerate(active)]
   202	            outs = llm.generate([{"prompt_token_ids": x["pid"] + x["resp"]} for x in active], sp, lora_request=lora,
   203	                                use_tqdm=False)
   204	            for x, o in zip(active, outs):
   205	                c = o.outputs[0]
   206	                ids = list(c.token_ids)
   207	                ended = bool(ids) and ids[-1] == im_end
   208	                if ended:
   209	                    ids = ids[:-1]
   210	                x["resp"] += ids
   211	                x["lab"] += ids
   212	                x["gen"] += len(ids)
   213	                if c.stop_reason == "<exec/>" or tok.decode(ids).rstrip().endswith("<exec/>"):
   214	                    fin, value, rec = R.clock_step(tok, x["p"]["question"], x["resp"], x["results"],
   215	                                                   x["round_start"])
   216	                    x["calls"] += 1
   217	                    if fin is None or x["gen"] >= args.max_total:
   218	                        x["done"] = True
   219	                        continue
   220	                    err = rec.get("error")
   221	                    x["errors"] += bool(err)
   222	                    if err and str(err).startswith("identical to call"):
   223	                        x["identical"] += 1
   224	                    if err:
   225	                        x["saw_error"] = True
   226	                    else:
   227	                        if x["saw_error"]:
   228	                            x["recovered"] = True
   229	                        x["written"].extend(r4_reward.name_rows(tok, rec.get("prog") or {}))
   230	                    x["results"].append(value)
   231	                    x["resp"] += fin
   232	                    x["lab"] += [-100] * len(fin)
   233	                    x["round_start"] = len(x["resp"])
   234	                    continue
   235	                x["done"], x["finished"] = True, ended or c.finish_reason == "stop"
   236	        gen_s = time.time() - g0
   237	        total_tokens = max(1, sum(x["gen"] for x in roll))
   238	        seqs, weights, stats = [], [], {"ok": [], "reward": [], "calls": [], "errors": [], "len": [], "by_src": {},
   239	                                        "call_by_src": {}, "row_frac": [], "identical": [], "recovered": []}
   240	        for gi in range(0, len(roll), args.group):
   241	            group = roll[gi:gi + args.group]
   242	            rs = []
   243	            for x in group:
   244	                text = tok.decode(x["resp"])
   245	                correct = bool(is_correct(x["p"], text))
   246	                ok = bool(x["finished"] and correct)
   247	                frac = r4_reward.row_fraction(r4_reward.reference_rows(x["p"].get("ex")), x["written"])
   248	                r = r4_reward.score(correct, x["finished"], x["gen"], x["identical"], x["recovered"], frac,
   249	                                    args.len_cost)
   250	                rs.append(r)
   251	                stats["row_frac"].append(frac)
   252	                stats["identical"].append(x["identical"])
   253	                stats["recovered"].append(bool(x["recovered"]))
   254	                src = x["p"]["source"]
   255	                stats["ok"].append(ok)
   256	                stats["reward"].append(r)
   257	                stats["calls"].append(x["calls"])
   258	                stats["errors"].append(x["errors"])
   259	                stats["len"].append(x["gen"])
   260	                stats["by_src"].setdefault(src, []).append(ok)
   261	                stats["call_by_src"].setdefault(src, []).append(x["calls"] > 0)
   262	                if len(samples) < 300 and gi % (4 * args.group) == 0 and x is group[0] and it % 5 == 0:
   263	                    samples.append({"iter": it, "source": src, "question": x["p"]["question"][:2000],
   264	                                    "response": text[:8000], "ok": ok, "calls": x["calls"]})
   265	            mean = sum(rs) / len(rs)
   266	            for x, r in zip(group, rs):
   267	                if abs(r - mean) < 1e-9:
   268	                    continue
   269	                tail = [im_end] if x["finished"] else []
   270	                seqs.append((x["pid"] + x["resp"] + tail, [-100] * len(x["pid"]) + x["lab"] + tail))
   271	                weights.append((r - mean) / total_tokens)
   272	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
   273	        u0 = time.time()
   274	        pg = forward_logps(seqs, weights) if seqs else 0.0
   275	        # reference transcripts (supervised, executor of compile/quoting kept)
   276	        sft_seqs = []
   277	        while sum(len(s[0]) for s in sft_seqs) < args.sft_tokens:
   278	            t = rng.choice(D.TRAIN_TEMPLATES)
   279	            L = D.loguniform_int(rng, 2, 256 if t not in D.NUMBER_TEMPLATES else 100)
   280	            ex = D.example(tok, rng, t, L, "train", im_end=im_end)
   281	            ids, labels, _ = R.build(tok, ex["question"], D.segments_ids(tok, ex), im_end)
   282	            if len(ids) <= args.micro_tokens:
   283	                sft_seqs.append((ids, labels))
   284	        n_sft = sum(sum(1 for v in lab if v != -100) for _, lab in sft_seqs)
   285	        sft = forward_logps(sft_seqs, [args.sft_weight / n_sft] * len(sft_seqs)) / args.sft_weight
   286	        keep_seqs = []
   287	        while keep_pool and sum(len(s[0]) for s in keep_seqs) < args.keep_tokens:
   288	            item = keep_pool[rng.randrange(len(keep_pool))]
   289	            pair = tokenize(tok, {"prompt": item["prompt"]}, [(item["response"], True)], im_end)
   290	            if len(pair[0]) <= args.micro_tokens:
   291	                keep_seqs.append(pair)
   292	        keep = 0.0
   293	        if keep_seqs:
   294	            n_keep = sum(sum(1 for v in lab if v != -100) for _, lab in keep_seqs)
   295	            keep = forward_logps(keep_seqs, [args.keep_weight / n_keep] * len(keep_seqs)) / args.keep_weight
   296	        pin_exs = sorted((pb_pin.pinned_example(pin_rng, pool, "train", 16) for _ in range(args.pinned_tables)),
   297	                         key=lambda e: len(e["pi"]))
   298	        pin = packed(pb_pin.packed_batch, pin_exs, 16, args.pinned_tables * 16)
   299	        me_exs = sorted((mealy_example(mealy_rng, tok, pool, "train", 16, 0.15) for _ in range(args.mealy_tables)),
   300	                        key=lambda e: len(e["pi"]))
   301	        me = packed(AD.mealy_packed_batch, me_exs, 8, args.mealy_tables * 32)
   302	        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable, 1.0)
   303	        mult = r4_reward._multiplier(it, args.iters, args.warmup) if args.schedule == "cosine" else 1.0
   304	        lr_now = args.lr * mult
   305	        for group in opt.param_groups:
   306	            group["lr"] = lr_now
   307	        opt.step()
   308	        rec = {"iter": it, "acc": round(sum(stats["ok"]) / len(stats["ok"]), 4),
   309	               "reward": round(sum(stats["reward"]) / len(stats["reward"]), 4),
   310	               "calls_mean": round(sum(stats["calls"]) / len(stats["calls"]), 3),
   311	               "errors_mean": round(sum(stats["errors"]) / len(stats["errors"]), 3),
   312	               "len_mean": round(sum(stats["len"]) / len(stats["len"]), 1),
   313	               "acc_by_source": {k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 3) for k, v in sorted(stats["by_src"].items())},
   314	               "call_rate_by_source": {k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 3) for k, v in sorted(stats["call_by_src"].items())},
   315	               "row_frac": round(sum(stats["row_frac"]) / len(stats["row_frac"]), 4),
   316	               "identical_mean": round(sum(stats["identical"]) / len(stats["identical"]), 3),
   317	               "recovered_rate": round(sum(stats["recovered"]) / len(stats["recovered"]), 3),
   318	               "lr": lr_now,
   319	               "pg_loss": pg, "sft_loss_per_token": round(sft, 5), "keep_loss_per_token": round(keep, 5),
   320	               "pinned_loss": round(pin, 5), "mealy_loss": round(me, 5), "grad_norm": float(gn),
   321	               "updated_seqs": len(seqs), "gen_s": round(gen_s, 1), "update_s": round(time.time() - u0, 1),
   322	               "elapsed": round(time.time() - t0, 1)}
   323	        log.append(rec)
   324	        print(json.dumps(rec), flush=True)
   325	        if (it + 1) % args.save_every == 0 or it + 1 == args.iters:
   326	            model.save_pretrained(out / f"adapter_iter{it + 1}")
   327	            atomic_json(out / "rl_record.json", {"args": vars(args), "log": log, "partial": it + 1 < args.iters,
   328	                                                 "host": host_record(), "at": stamp()})
   329	            atomic_json(out / "rl_samples.json", {"samples": samples})
   330	    atomic_json(out / "rl_record.json", {"args": vars(args), "log": log, "partial": False,
   331	                                         "wall": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "host": host_record(), "finished_at": stamp()})
   332	
   333	
   334	if __name__ == "__main__":
   335	    main()
     1	"""
     2	Registered GRPO reward for r4 (R4.md, before any RL spend).
     3	
     4	    1.0    the final answer is correct and the response ended
     5	    +0.25  times the fraction of reference rows present in the written tables
     6	           (tape: (state, event, destination) names; number mode: the 5-token row).
     7	           0 when the prompt has no reference table
     8	    -0.2   per `identical to call` error
     9	    -len_cost per 1,000 generated tokens (0.01, PROMPTBOX's value)
    10	    +0.1   if any call succeeds after an error
    11	
    12	Arm B's learning rate is the unique constant that makes the cosine schedule sum to 15 times PROMPTBOX's
    13	summed learning rate (60 steps at 1e-5).
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import math
    19	
    20	
    21	PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR = 60 * 1e-5
    22	
    23	
    24	def _multiplier(step, total, warmup):
    25	    if step < warmup:
    26	        return (step + 1) / warmup
    27	    frac = (step - warmup) / max(1, total - warmup)
    28	    return 0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * frac))
    29	
    30	
    31	def arm_b_lr(total=80, warmup=0, multiple=15.0):
    32	    """Learning rate whose schedule sums to `multiple` times PROMPTBOX's summed lr."""
    33	    scale = sum(_multiplier(s, total, warmup) for s in range(total))
    34	    return multiple * PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR / scale
    35	
    36	
    37	def schedule_sum(lr, total=80, warmup=0):
    38	    return sum(lr * _multiplier(s, total, warmup) for s in range(total))
    39	
    40	
    41	def score(correct, finished, n_tokens, n_identical, recovered, row_frac, len_cost=0.01):
    42	    value = 1.0 if correct and finished else 0.0
    43	    value += 0.25 * row_frac
    44	    value -= 0.2 * n_identical
    45	    value -= len_cost * n_tokens / 1000.0
    46	    value += 0.1 if recovered else 0.0
    47	    return value
    48	
    49	
    50	def name_rows(tok, prog):
    51	    """Written rows as name tuples. Tape rows are 3 names; number-mode rows are 5."""
    52	    out = []
    53	    for reg in prog.get("registers") or []:
    54	        width = 5 if reg.get("kind") == "mealy" else 3
    55	        for row in reg.get("rows") or []:
    56	            if len(row) < width:
    57	                continue
    58	            out.append(tuple(tok.decode([t]).strip() for t in row[:width]))
    59	    return out
    60	
    61	
    62	def row_fraction(reference, written):
    63	    if not reference:
    64	        return 0.0
    65	    got = set(written)
    66	    return sum(1 for row in reference if tuple(row) in got) / len(reference)
    67	
    68	
    69	def reference_rows(ex):
    70	    """Gold table rows from a composite example. Empty when the prompt has no reference table."""
    71	    if not ex:
    72	        return []
    73	    rows = []
    74	    for kind, spec in ex.get("segments") or []:
    75	        if kind != "call":
    76	            continue
    77	        for reg in ((spec or {}).get("lit") or {}).get("registers") or []:
    78	            rows.extend(tuple(row) for row in reg.get("rows") or [])
    79	    return rows
    80	
    81	
    82	def selftest():
    83	    assert abs(schedule_sum(arm_b_lr()) - 15 * PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR) < 1e-12
    84	    assert abs(40 * 3e-4 - 20 * PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR) < 1e-12
    85	    assert abs(score(True, True, 1000, 0, False, 0.0) - 0.99) < 1e-12
    86	    assert abs(score(True, True, 1000, 0, False, 1.0) - 1.24) < 1e-12
    87	    assert abs(score(True, True, 1000, 1, False, 1.0) - 1.04) < 1e-12
    88	    assert abs(score(False, True, 0, 0, True, 0.0) - 0.1) < 1e-12
    89	    assert score(True, False, 0, 0, False, 0.0) == 0.0
    90	    import random
    91	    import sys
    92	    import pathlib
    93	    here = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    94	    for p in (here.parent / "reason", here.parent / "arith", here.parent / "promptbox"):
    95	        if str(p) not in sys.path:
    96	            sys.path.insert(0, str(p))
    97	    import reason_core as R
    98	    import reason_data as D
    99	    tok = R.load_tokenizer()
   100	    im = tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   101	    ex = D.example(tok, random.Random(0), "shelf_bill", 8, "train", im_end=im)
   102	    ref, written = [], []
   103	    for kind, spec in ex["segments"]:
   104	        if kind != "call":
   105	            continue
   106	        ref.extend(tuple(row) for reg in spec["lit"]["registers"] for row in reg["rows"])
   107	        written.extend(name_rows(tok, R.parse_call(tok, D.spec_ids(tok, spec))))
   108	    assert reference_rows(ex) == ref
   109	    assert reference_rows(None) == []
   110	    assert row_fraction(ref, written) == 1.0, written[:3]
   111	    assert row_fraction([], written) == 0.0
   112	    return {"arm_b_lr": arm_b_lr(), "row_fraction": 1.0}
   113	
   114	
   115	if __name__ == "__main__":
   116	    import json
   117	    print(json.dumps(selftest()))
    42	class ReasonStream(Stream):
    43	    """PROMPTBOX's stream with ARITH's kinds and REASON's. A draw picks a kind from the full mix; PROMPTBOX's own
    44	    kinds are drawn by PROMPTBOX's `_one` with their weights renormalised, so every kind keeps its share."""
    45	
    46	    def __init__(self, tok, args, general, ordinary=(), negarith=()):
    47	        super().__init__(tok, args, general, ordinary)
    48	        self.negarith = negarith
    49	        self.full_mix = dict(self.mix)
    50	        rest = {k: v for k, v in self.mix.items() if k not in SPECIAL}
    51	        self.rest_mix = {k: v / sum(rest.values()) for k, v in rest.items()}
    52	        self.templates = [t for t in args.templates.split(",") if t]
    53	        self.weights = [float(w) for w in args.template_weights.split(",")] if args.template_weights else \
    54	            [1.0] * len(self.templates)
    55	        self.errors = 0
    56	
    57	    def _length(self, template, plain):
    58	        rng, a = self.rng, self.args
    59	        if template in RD.NUMBER_TEMPLATES:
    60	            hi = a.plain_max_digits if plain else (a.long_max_digits if rng.random() < a.long_frac else a.max_digits)
    61	            return RD.loguniform_int(rng, 1, hi)
    62	        if template in RD.SENTENCE_TEMPLATES:
    63	            hi = min(a.plain_max_L, a.sentence_max_L) if plain else a.sentence_max_L
    64	            if (not plain and getattr(a, "sentence_long_frac", 0)
    65	                    and rng.random() < a.sentence_long_frac):
    66	                hi = a.sentence_long_max
    67	            return RD.loguniform_int(rng, 2, hi)
    68	        hi = a.plain_max_L if plain else (a.long_max_L if rng.random() < a.long_frac else a.max_L_reason)
    69	        return RD.loguniform_int(rng, 2, hi)
    70	
    71	    def _reason(self, plain):
    72	        rng = self.rng
    73	        t = rng.choices(self.templates, weights=self.weights)[0]
    74	        L = self._length(t, plain)
    75	        kw = {}
    76	        if t == "parity_pick_sum":
    77	            kw["digits"] = RD.loguniform_int(rng, 1, self.args.plain_max_digits if plain else self.args.max_digits)
    78	        ex = RD.example(self.tok, rng, t, L, "train", im_end=self.im_end, **kw)
    79	        if not plain and rng.random() < self.args.error_frac:
    80	            bad = RD.with_error(self.tok, rng, ex, self.im_end)
    81	            if bad is not None:
    82	                self.errors += 1
    83	                ids, labels = bad["_built"]
    84	                return (ids, labels)
    85	        ids, labels, _ = RC.build(self.tok, ex["question"], RD.segments_ids(self.tok, ex), self.im_end, plain=plain)
    86	        return ids, labels
    87	
    88	    def _extra(self, kind):
    89	        """Override in a subclass to add mix kinds. Return (ids, labels) or None to use the base kinds."""
    90	        return None
    91	
    92	    def _one(self):
    93	        rng = self.rng
    94	        kind = rng.choices(list(self.full_mix), weights=list(self.full_mix.values()))[0]
    95	        extra = self._extra(kind)
    96	        if extra is not None:
    97	            return kind, extra, extra
    98	        if kind in ("reason", "reason_plain"):
    99	            pair = self._reason(kind == "reason_plain")
   100	            return kind, pair, pair
   101	        if kind == "sub":
   102	            ex = RD.sub_example(self.tok, rng, "train", max_L=self.args.max_digits, im_end=self.im_end)
   103	            ids, labels, _ = RC.build(self.tok, ex["question"], RD.segments_ids(self.tok, ex), self.im_end)
   104	            return kind, (ids, labels), (ids, labels)
   105	        if kind == "arith":
   106	            ex = AD.arith_example(rng, max_L=self.args.arith_max_L, split="train", long_frac=self.args.arith_long_frac,
   107	                                  long_max_L=self.args.arith_long_max_L)
   108	            pair = AD.arith_ids(self.tok, ex, self.im_end)
   109	            return kind, pair, pair
   110	        if kind == "negarith":
   111	            item = self.negarith[rng.randrange(len(self.negarith))]
   112	            pair = tokenize(self.tok, {"prompt": item["prompt"]}, [(item["response"], True)], self.im_end)
   113	            return kind, pair, pair
   114	        self.mix = self.rest_mix
   115	        try:
   116	            return super()._one()
   117	        finally:
   118	            self.mix = self.full_mix
   119	
   120	    def batch(self, budget):
   121	        out, used = [], 0
   122	        while used < budget:
   123	            kind, full, train = self._one()
   124	            if len(full[0]) > self.args.max_tokens:
   125	                self.counts["_too_long_" + kind] = self.counts.get("_too_long_" + kind, 0) + 1
   126	                continue
   127	            self.counts[kind] = self.counts.get(kind, 0) + 1
   128	            self.counts["_tokens_" + kind] = self.counts.get("_tokens_" + kind, 0) + len(full[0])
   129	            out.append((*train, kind in KL_KINDS or kind == "negarith"))
   130	            used += len(full[0])
   131	        return out
   132	
   133	
   134	STREAM_CLS = ReasonStream
   135	
   136	
   171	def main():
   172	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   173	    ap.add_argument("--model", default="Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
   174	    ap.add_argument("--stage", default="reason")
   175	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   176	    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=500)
   177	    ap.add_argument("--tokens_per_step", type=int, default=16384)
   178	    ap.add_argument("--micro_tokens", type=int, default=16384)
   179	    ap.add_argument("--max_tokens", type=int, default=20000)
   180	    ap.add_argument("--max_L", type=int, default=2048)
   181	    ap.add_argument("--arith_max_L", type=int, default=400)
   182	    ap.add_argument("--arith_long_frac", type=float, default=0.0, help="share of arith draws with long operands")
   183	    ap.add_argument("--arith_long_max_L", type=int, default=4500)
   184	    ap.add_argument("--negarith_file", default="")
   185	    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
   186	    ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=20)
   187	    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1)
   188	    ap.add_argument("--mix", required=True)
   189	    ap.add_argument("--answer_only", action="store_true")
   190	    ap.add_argument("--verify_base", default="interp,family")
   191	    ap.add_argument("--init_adapter", required=True)
   192	    ap.add_argument("--general_file", required=True)
   193	    ap.add_argument("--ordinary_file", required=True)
   194	    ap.add_argument("--kl_coef", type=float, default=1.0)
   195	    ap.add_argument("--ckpts", default="1.0")
   196	    ap.add_argument("--pinned_tables", type=int, default=16)
   197	    ap.add_argument("--pinned_blocks", type=int, default=16)
   198	    ap.add_argument("--pinned_micro", type=int, default=16)
   199	    ap.add_argument("--mealy_tables", type=int, default=16)
   200	    ap.add_argument("--mealy_blocks", type=int, default=16)
   201	    ap.add_argument("--mealy_micro", type=int, default=8)
   202	    ap.add_argument("--family_exclude", default="parity,cups,robot,compass,cycle,prose,addition,weboflies,tracking")
   203	    ap.add_argument("--templates", default=",".join(RD.TRAIN_TEMPLATES))
   204	    ap.add_argument("--template_weights", default="")
   205	    ap.add_argument("--max_L_reason", type=int, default=2048, help="tape templates: events, log-uniform 2 …")
   206	    ap.add_argument("--long_max_L", type=int, default=4096)
   207	    ap.add_argument("--sentence_max_L", type=int, default=1024)
   208	    ap.add_argument("--sentence_long_max", type=int, default=4096)
   209	    ap.add_argument("--sentence_long_frac", type=float, default=0.0,
   210	                    help="share of sentence draws trained out to --sentence_long_max")
   211	    ap.add_argument("--repair_file", default="", help="on-policy repair items (r4)")
   212	    ap.add_argument("--max_digits", type=int, default=400)
   213	    ap.add_argument("--long_max_digits", type=int, default=1200)
   214	    ap.add_argument("--long_frac", type=float, default=0.15)
   215	    ap.add_argument("--plain_max_L", type=int, default=512)
   216	    ap.add_argument("--plain_max_digits", type=int, default=100)
   217	    ap.add_argument("--error_frac", type=float, default=0.04)
   218	    ap.add_argument("--mealy_sub_frac", type=float, default=0.15)
   219	    ap.add_argument("--pinned_mode_frac", type=float, default=0.75)
   220	    args = ap.parse_args()
   221	    if "addition" not in args.family_exclude.split(","):
   222	        raise SystemExit("addition must be excluded from the rule-style family kind")
   223	
   224	    import torch
   225	    from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
   226	    from peft import PeftModel
   227	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   228	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
   229	
   230	    import pb_pin
   231	
   232	    out = pathlib.Path(args.out)
   233	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   234	    hf_tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)
   235	    probe = hf_tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
   236	                                       enable_thinking=False)
   237	    assert probe == chat_prompt("hi") == AD.chat_prompt("hi"), probe
   238	    tok = Tokenizer.from_file(str(pathlib.Path(snapshot_download(args.model)) / "tokenizer.json"))
   239	    general_all = json.loads(pathlib.Path(args.general_file).read_text())["items"]
   240	    general = [g for g in general_all if not AD.looks_like_addition(g["prompt"])]
   241	    ordinary = json.loads(pathlib.Path(args.ordinary_file).read_text())["items"]
   242	    dropped = len(general_all) - len(general)
   243	    print(json.dumps({"general": len(general), "general_dropped_as_addition": dropped, "ordinary": len(ordinary)}),
   244	          flush=True)
   245	    negarith = json.loads(pathlib.Path(args.negarith_file).read_text())["items"] if args.negarith_file else []
   246	    if "negarith" in args.mix and not negarith:
   247	        raise SystemExit("mix asks for negarith data but no --negarith_file")
   248	    print(json.dumps({"negarith": len(negarith)}), flush=True)
   249	    stream = STREAM_CLS(tok, args, general, ordinary, negarith)
   250	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
   251	    pin_rng, mealy_rng = random.Random(args.seed + 7919), random.Random(args.seed + 104729)
   252	    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
   253	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(args.model, dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation="sdpa").cuda()
   254	    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
   255	    model.enable_input_require_grads()
   256	    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.init_adapter, is_trainable=True)
   257	    trainable = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
   258	    n_train = sum(p.numel() for p in trainable)
   259	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(trainable, lr=args.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.99), weight_decay=0.0)
   260	    total = args.steps
   261	    ckpt_steps = sorted({max(1, int(round(float(f) * total))) for f in args.ckpts.split(",")})
   262	
   263	    def lr_at(step):
   264	        if step < args.warmup:
   265	            return args.lr * (step + 1) / args.warmup
   266	        frac = (step - args.warmup) / max(1, total - args.warmup)
   267	        return args.lr * (0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * frac)))
   268	
   269	    def packed_loss(batch_fn, exs, micro, n_labels):
   270	        tot, ok = 0.0, 0
   271	        for k in range(0, len(exs), micro):
   272	            ids, pos, allow, r, c, lab = batch_fn(exs[k:k + micro], stream.im_end)
   273	            hidden = core.model(input_ids=ids.cuda(), position_ids=pos.cuda(), attention_mask=allow.cuda()).last_hidden_state
   274	            loss, n_ok = chunked_ce(core.lm_head, hidden[r.cuda(), c.cuda()], lab.cuda())
   275	            (loss / n_labels).backward()
   276	            tot += loss.item()
   277	            ok += n_ok
   278	            del hidden, loss
   279	        return tot, ok
   280	
   281	    core = model.base_model.model
   282	    log, t0, tokens_seen, saved = [], time.time(), 0, []
   283	    mealy_kinds = {}
   284	    model.train()
   285	    for step in range(total):
   286	        exs = stream.batch(args.tokens_per_step)
   287	        exs.sort(key=lambda e: len(e[0]))
   288	        micro, cur, cur_max = [], [], 0
   289	        for e in exs:
   290	            L = len(e[0])
   291	            if cur and max(cur_max, L) * (len(cur) + 1) > args.micro_tokens:
   292	                micro.append(cur)
   293	                cur, cur_max = [], 0
   294	            cur.append(e)
   295	            cur_max = max(cur_max, L)
   296	        if cur:
   297	            micro.append(cur)
   298	        n_target = max(1, sum(sum(1 for x in lab if x != -100) for _, lab, _ in exs))
   299	        for g in opt.param_groups:
   300	            g["lr"] = lr_at(step)
   301	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
   302	        loss_sum, correct, kl_sum, kl_n = 0.0, 0, 0.0, 0
   303	        pin_n = args.pinned_tables * args.pinned_blocks
   304	        pin_exs = sorted([pb_pin.pinned_example(pin_rng, pool, "train", args.pinned_blocks)
   305	                          for _ in range(args.pinned_tables)], key=lambda ex: len(ex["pi"]))
   306	        pin_loss, pin_ok = packed_loss(pb_pin.packed_batch, pin_exs, args.pinned_micro, max(1, pin_n))
   307	        me_n = args.mealy_tables * args.mealy_blocks * 2
   308	        me_exs = sorted([mealy_example(mealy_rng, tok, pool, "train", args.mealy_blocks, args.mealy_sub_frac)
   309	                         for _ in range(args.mealy_tables)], key=lambda ex: len(ex["pi"]))
   310	        for ex in me_exs:
   311	            mealy_kinds[ex["kind"]] = mealy_kinds.get(ex["kind"], 0) + 1
   312	        me_loss, me_ok = packed_loss(AD.mealy_packed_batch, me_exs, args.mealy_micro, max(1, me_n))
   313	        for mb in micro:
   314	            L = max(len(ids) for ids, _, _ in mb)
   315	            ids_t = torch.full((len(mb), L), stream.im_end, dtype=torch.long)
   316	            lab_t = torch.full((len(mb), L), -100, dtype=torch.long)
   317	            for i, (ids, lab, _) in enumerate(mb):
   318	                ids_t[i, :len(ids)] = torch.tensor(ids)
   319	                lab_t[i, :len(lab)] = torch.tensor(lab)
   320	                tokens_seen += len(ids)
   321	            ids_t, lab_t = ids_t.cuda(), lab_t.cuda()
   322	            hidden = core.model(input_ids=ids_t).last_hidden_state[:, :-1]
   323	            tgt = lab_t[:, 1:]
   324	            mask = tgt != -100
   325	            loss, n_ok = chunked_ce(core.lm_head, hidden[mask], tgt[mask])
   326	            total_loss = loss
   327	            kl_rows = torch.tensor([is_kl for _, _, is_kl in mb], device=ids_t.device)
   328	            if args.kl_coef and bool(kl_rows.any()):
   329	                with torch.no_grad(), model.disable_adapter():
   330	                    hb = core.model(input_ids=ids_t[kl_rows]).last_hidden_state[:, :-1]
   331	                km = mask[kl_rows]
   332	                kl = chunked_kl(core.lm_head, hidden[kl_rows][km], hb[km])
   333	                total_loss = total_loss + args.kl_coef * kl
   334	                kl_sum += kl.item()
   335	                kl_n += int(km.sum())
   336	                del hb, kl
   337	            (total_loss / n_target).backward()
   338	            loss_sum += loss.item()
   339	            correct += n_ok
   340	            del hidden, loss, total_loss
   341	        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable, 1.0)
   342	        opt.step()
   343	        if step % 10 == 0 or step == total - 1:
   344	            rec = {"step": step, "loss": round(loss_sum / n_target, 5), "token_acc": round(correct / n_target, 5),
   345	                   "lr": lr_at(step), "grad_norm": round(float(gn), 4), "examples": len(exs),
   346	                   "elapsed": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "tokens_per_s": round(tokens_seen / (time.time() - t0), 1),
   347	                   "counts": dict(stream.counts), "pinned_loss": round(pin_loss / max(1, pin_n), 5),
   348	                   "pinned_acc": round(pin_ok / max(1, pin_n), 5), "mealy_loss": round(me_loss / max(1, me_n), 5),
   349	                   "mealy_acc": round(me_ok / max(1, me_n), 5), "mealy_kinds": dict(mealy_kinds)}
   350	            if kl_n:
   351	                rec.update(kl_per_token=round(kl_sum / kl_n, 5), kl_tokens=kl_n)
   352	            log.append(rec)
   353	            print(json.dumps(rec), flush=True)
   354	        if step + 1 in ckpt_steps:
   355	            path = out / f"adapter_step{step + 1}"
   356	            model.save_pretrained(path)
   357	            saved.append(str(path))
   358	            atomic_json(out / "train_record.json", {"args": vars(args), "trainable_params": n_train, "log": log,
   359	                                                     "saved": saved, "partial": step + 1 < total,
   360	                                                     "general_dropped_as_addition": dropped, "host": host_record(),
   361	                                                     "at": stamp()})
   362	    atomic_json(out / "train_record.json", {"args": vars(args), "trainable_params": n_train, "log": log, "saved": saved,
   363	                                             "partial": False, "wall": round(time.time() - t0, 1),
   364	                                             "general_dropped_as_addition": dropped, "host": host_record(),
   365	                                             "finished_at": stamp(), "example_counts": stream.counts,
   366	                                             "error_examples": stream.errors,

     1	"""
     2	Is the language model still intact? Perplexity on WikiText-2 (test) and HumanEval (prompt + canonical solution), and
     3	accuracy on fixed MMLU and GSM8K slices, in non-thinking mode with greedy decoding. Run once for the base model and
     4	once per fine-tuned model; the report compares them.
     5	
     6	    python pb_lmeval.py --model Qwen/Qwen3-4B [--adapter dir] --out <dir> --tag base
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import argparse
    12	import math
    13	import pathlib
    14	import random
    15	import re
    16	import sys
    17	import time
    18	
    19	sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
    20	from pb_common import atomic_json, host_record, interval_record, stamp  # noqa: E402
    21	from pb_data import chat_prompt  # noqa: E402
    22	from pb_eval import merge_adapter  # noqa: E402
    23	
    24	WINDOW = 2048
    25	
    26	
    27	def windows(ids, size=WINDOW):
    28	    return [ids[i:i + size] for i in range(0, len(ids) - 1, size) if len(ids[i:i + size]) > 16]
    29	
    30	
    31	def nll_of(llm, seqs):
    32	    from vllm import SamplingParams
    33	    outs = llm.generate([{"prompt_token_ids": s} for s in seqs],
    34	                        SamplingParams(max_tokens=1, prompt_logprobs=0, temperature=0.0))
    35	    total, count = 0.0, 0
    36	    for s, o in zip(seqs, outs):
    37	        for tid, lp in zip(s[1:], o.prompt_logprobs[1:]):
    38	            total -= lp[tid].logprob
    39	            count += 1
    40	    return total, count
    41	
    42	
    43	def main():
    44	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    45	    ap.add_argument("--model", default="Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
    46	    ap.add_argument("--adapter", default="")
    47	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    48	    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="base")
    49	    ap.add_argument("--mmlu_per_subject", type=int, default=20)
    50	    ap.add_argument("--gsm8k_n", type=int, default=400)
    51	    ap.add_argument("--ban_program", action="store_true",
    52	                    help="diagnostic only: forbid `<program>` in MMLU and GSM8K decoding (never a registered score)")
    53	    ap.add_argument("--ban_tags", action="store_true",
    54	                    help="diagnostic only: forbid every non-special token containing `<` (never a registered score)")
    55	    args = ap.parse_args()
    56	    from datasets import load_dataset
    57	    from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
    58	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
    59	    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    60	
    61	    t0 = time.time()
    62	    tok = Tokenizer.from_file(str(pathlib.Path(snapshot_download(args.model)) / "tokenizer.json"))
    63	    path = args.model
    64	    if args.adapter:
    65	        path = "/tmp/pb_lmeval_merged"
    66	        merge_adapter(args.model, args.adapter, path)
    67	    llm = LLM(model=path, tokenizer=args.model, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.88,
    68	              seed=0)
    69	    res = {}
    70	    wiki = "\n\n".join(r["text"] for r in load_dataset("Salesforce/wikitext", "wikitext-2-raw-v1", split="test"))
    71	    seqs = windows(tok.encode(wiki, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
    72	    tot, cnt = nll_of(llm, seqs)
    73	    res["wikitext2"] = {"nll_per_token": tot / cnt, "ppl": math.exp(tot / cnt), "tokens": cnt, "windows": len(seqs)}
    74	    he = load_dataset("openai/openai_humaneval", split="test")
    75	    seqs = [tok.encode(r["prompt"] + r["canonical_solution"], add_special_tokens=False).ids for r in he]
    76	    tot, cnt = nll_of(llm, seqs)
    77	    res["humaneval"] = {"nll_per_token": tot / cnt, "ppl": math.exp(tot / cnt), "tokens": cnt, "problems": len(seqs)}
    78	
    79	    mmlu = load_dataset("cais/mmlu", "all", split="test")
    80	    by_subj = {}
    81	    for r in mmlu:
    82	        by_subj.setdefault(r["subject"], []).append(r)
    83	    rng = random.Random(5)
    84	    picked = []
    85	    for subj in sorted(by_subj):
    86	        rows = by_subj[subj]
    87	        picked += rng.sample(rows, min(args.mmlu_per_subject, len(rows)))
    88	    prompts = []
    89	    for r in picked:
    90	        opts = "\n".join(f"{'ABCD'[i]}. {c}" for i, c in enumerate(r["choices"]))
    91	        prompts.append(chat_prompt(f"{r['question']}\n\n{opts}\n\nReply with the letter of the correct answer only."))
    92	    # `<program` is the prefix shared by `<program>` and `<program>\n` (one token for `>\n`)
    93	    ban = {"bad_words": ["<program"]} if args.ban_program else {}
    94	    if args.ban_tags:
    95	        # `--ban_program` was evaded by writing `< program >`; this closes every spelling
    96	        ends = {tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>"), tok.token_to_id("<|endoftext|>")}
    97	        special = {t.content for t in tok.get_added_tokens_decoder().values()}
    98	        ban = {"logit_bias": {i: -100.0 for t, i in tok.get_vocab().items()
    99	                              if i not in ends and ("<" in t or t in special)}}
   100	    outs = llm.generate(prompts, SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=8, **ban))
   101	    ok, mmlu_texts = 0, []
   102	    for r, o in zip(picked, outs):
   103	        m = re.search(r"[ABCD]", o.outputs[0].text)
   104	        ok += bool(m) and "ABCD".index(m.group(0)) == r["answer"]
   105	        mmlu_texts.append(o.outputs[0].text)
   106	    res["mmlu"] = interval_record(ok, len(picked))
   107	    res["mmlu_program_rate"] = sum(t.lstrip().startswith("<") for t in mmlu_texts) / len(mmlu_texts)
   108	    res["mmlu_no_letter_rate"] = sum(not re.search(r"[ABCD]", t) for t in mmlu_texts) / len(mmlu_texts)
   109	
   110	    gsm = load_dataset("openai/gsm8k", "main", split="test")
   111	    rows = list(gsm)[: args.gsm8k_n]
   112	    prompts = [chat_prompt(r["question"] + "\nPlease reason step by step, and put your final answer within \\boxed{}.")
   113	               for r in rows]
   114	    outs = llm.generate(prompts, SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=1536, **ban))
   115	    ok, ok_last, texts = 0, 0, []
   116	    for r, o in zip(rows, outs):
   117	        gold = r["answer"].split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")
   118	        text = o.outputs[0].text
   119	        boxes = re.findall(r"\\boxed\{([^{}]*)\}", text)
   120	        got = boxes[-1].replace(",", "").replace("$", "").strip() if boxes else ""
   121	        nums = re.findall(r"-?\d[\d,]*\.?\d*", text)
   122	        last = nums[-1].replace(",", "").rstrip(".") if nums else ""
   123	        for val, name in ((got, "boxed"), (last, "last")):
   124	            try:
   125	                hit = abs(float(val) - float(gold)) < 1e-6
   126	            except ValueError:
   127	                hit = False
   128	            if name == "boxed":
   129	                ok += hit
   130	            else:
   131	                ok_last += hit
   132	        texts.append({"question": r["question"], "gold": gold, "response": text})
   133	    res["gsm8k"] = interval_record(ok, len(rows))
   134	    res["gsm8k_last_number"] = interval_record(ok_last, len(rows))
   135	    res["gsm8k_program_rate"] = sum("<program>" in x["response"] for x in texts) / len(texts)
   136	    res["gsm8k_boxed_rate"] = sum("\\boxed" in x["response"] for x in texts) / len(texts)
   137	    atomic_json(pathlib.Path(args.out) / f"lm_{args.tag}_gsm8k_texts.json", {"items": texts, "mmlu": mmlu_texts})
   138	    print(res, flush=True)
   139	    atomic_json(pathlib.Path(args.out) / f"lm_{args.tag}.json", {"args": vars(args), "results": res,
   140	                                                                 "host": host_record(), "wall": round(time.time() - t0, 1),
   141	                                                                 "finished_at": stamp()})
   142	
   143	
   144	if __name__ == "__main__":
   145	    main()

     1	"""
     2	COMPILE problems for the loop, the base model and the frontier (COMPILE.md §5): the same questions for every model,
     3	one seeded stream per (cell, arm, length), and the hooks that let REASON's loop, base and frontier scripts run them.
     4	
     5	Cell spec (reason_loop.parse_cells syntax, `cell:arm:lengths[:n]`):
     6	    iid|tmpl|kind|style|all3 : tape|num : lengths     the generator's cells (events, or digits for num)
     7	    wild : all | <family>    : lengths                  compile_wild's hand-written families (n per family)
     8	    legacy : elevator|clock|pattern : lengths           PROMPTBOX's held-out families, half each phrasing split
     9	    gentest : tape : lengths                            PROMPTBOX's pb_gen test pools
    10	
    11	`patch()` installs into reason_loop: make_problems, correct (the answer key on the text after the last final, or the
    12	`Answer:` line) and call_vs_ref (compile = tables exact up to renaming, reading = tape or numbers exact); and into
    13	reason_core: parse_call = compile_core.parse_call (use lines). Without use lines the parser is REASON's (selftest).
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import pathlib
    19	import random
    20	import sys
    21	
    22	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    23	for _p in (HERE, HERE.parent / "reason", HERE.parent / "arith", HERE.parent / "promptbox"):
    24	    if str(_p) not in sys.path:
    25	        sys.path.insert(0, str(_p))
    26	import compile_core as C  # noqa: E402
    27	import compile_gen as G  # noqa: E402
    28	import compile_wild as W  # noqa: E402
    29	import pb_lit  # noqa: E402
    30	import reason_core as R  # noqa: E402
    31	from pb_families import answer_correct, answer_key, make  # noqa: E402
    32	from pb_lang import parse_program, render_answer, run_program  # noqa: E402
    33	
    34	_V = {}
    35	
    36	
    37	def vocab(tok):
    38	    if "v" not in _V:
    39	        _V["v"] = G.Vocab(tok)
    40	    return _V["v"]
    41	
    42	
    43	def _ref(ex):
    44	    return {"arm": ex["arm"], "question": ex["question"], "truth": ex["truth"]}
    45	
    46	
    47	def _problem(cell, ex, L, extra=None):
    48	    p = {"cell": cell, "template": cell.split("|")[0], "split": cell.split("|")[1], "L": L, "question": ex["question"],
    49	         "answer": ex["answer"], "key": ex["key"], "kind": ex.get("kind"), "style": ex.get("style"),
    50	         "lib_eligible": any(ex.get("lib_eligible") or []), "ref_calls": [_ref(ex)], "answer_kind": "key"}
    51	    p.update(extra or {})
    52	    return p
    53	
    54	
    55	def legacy_example(tok, rng, family, L, split):
    56	    for _ in range(50):
    57	        inst = make(family, rng, L, split=split)
    58	        ex = {"question": inst.question, "prog_text": inst.program_text, "items": inst.items,
    59	              "prompt": R.chat_prompt(inst.question), "answer": None}
    60	        lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
    61	        if lit is None:
    62	            continue
    63	        prog = parse_program(inst.program_text)
    64	        steps, final = run_program(prog, inst.items)
    65	        answer = render_answer(prog, final, steps)
    66	        return {"arm": "tape", "kind": family, "style": split, "question": inst.question, "answer": answer,
    67	                "key": answer_key(prog, final, answer), "lib_eligible": [],
    68	                "truth": {"registers": [{"kind": "moore", "start": r["start"], "rows": r["rows"]}
    69	                                        for r in lit["registers"]], "tape": lit["tape"]}}
    70	    raise RuntimeError(f"no literal {family} at L={L}")
    71	
    72	
    73	def gentest_example(tok, rng, L):
    74	    import pb_gen
    75	    from pb_families import answer_key as ak
    76	    V = pb_gen.Vocab(tok)
    77	    ex, lit = pb_gen.example(rng, V, tok, L, "test")
    78	    prog = parse_program(ex["prog_text"])
    79	    steps, final = run_program(prog, ex["items"])
    80	    answer = render_answer(prog, final, steps)
    81	    return {"arm": "tape", "kind": "gen:test", "style": "gen", "question": ex["question"], "answer": answer,
    82	            "key": ak(prog, final, answer), "lib_eligible": [],
    83	            "truth": {"registers": [{"kind": "moore", "start": r["start"], "rows": r["rows"]} for r in lit["registers"]],
    84	                      "tape": lit["tape"]}}
    85	
    86	
    87	def make_problems(tok, cells, seed, mixed_digits=None):
    88	    """cells: reason_loop.parse_cells output, [(cell, arm, lengths, n)]."""
    89	    probs = []
    90	    V = None
    91	    for t, split, lengths, n in cells:
    92	        for L in lengths:
    93	            rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|{t}|{split}|{L}")
    94	            cell = f"{t}|{split}|L{L}"
    95	            if t in G.AXES:
    96	                for _ in range(n):
    97	                    if split == "num":
    98	                        ex, why = G.number_example(rng, tok, L, t)
    99	                    else:
   100	                        V = V or vocab(tok)
   101	                        ex, why = G.example(rng, V, tok, L, t)
   102	                    if ex is None:
   103	                        raise RuntimeError(f"{cell}: {why}")
   104	                    probs.append(_problem(cell, ex, L))
   105	            elif t == "wild":
   106	                fams = W.FAMILIES if split == "all" else [f for f in W.FAMILIES if f.__name__ == f"fam_{split}"]
   107	                for fn in fams:
   108	                    for _ in range(n):
   109	                        ex, why = W.example(rng, tok, fn, L)
   110	                        if ex is None:
   111	                            raise RuntimeError(f"{cell}/{fn.__name__}: {why}")
   112	                        probs.append(_problem(cell, ex, L, {"family": ex["family"], "held_kind": ex["held"]["kind"]}))
   113	            elif t == "legacy":
   114	                for j in range(n):
   115	                    sp = "train" if j % 2 == 0 else "heldout_phrasing"
   116	                    ex = legacy_example(tok, rng, split, L, sp)
   117	                    probs.append(_problem(cell, ex, L, {"phrasing_split": sp}))
   118	            elif t == "gentest":
   119	                for _ in range(n):
   120	                    probs.append(_problem(cell, gentest_example(tok, rng, L), L))
   121	            else:
   122	                raise ValueError(f"unknown cell {t}")
   123	    return probs
   124	
   125	
   126	def answer_text(text):
   127	    """The text after the last final (ours), or after </think>; an `Answer:` line if there is one."""
   128	    import reason_loop as RL
   129	    region, _ = RL.answer_region(text)
   130	    return region
   131	
   132	
   133	def correct(prob, text):
   134	    return answer_correct(prob["key"], answer_text(text))
   135	
   136	
   137	def call_vs_ref(tok, prog, reading, ref):
   138	    if ref is None or prog is None or prog.get("error"):
   139	        return None, None
   140	    truth = C.truth_regs_ids(tok, {"truth": ref["truth"]})
   141	    comp = C.tables_exact(prog, truth)
   142	    if reading is None:
   143	        return comp, False
   144	    if prog["mode"] == "tape":
   145	        want = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, e) for e in ref["truth"]["tape"]]
   146	        return comp, reading.get("tape") == want
   147	    return comp, list(reading.get("numbers") or []) == [ref["truth"]["first"], ref["truth"]["second"]]
   148	
   149	
   150	def patch():
   151	    import reason_loop as RL
   152	    R.parse_call = C.parse_call
   153	    RL.make_problems = make_problems
   154	    RL.correct = correct
   155	    RL.call_vs_ref = call_vs_ref
   156	    return RL
   157	
   158	
   159	def library_use(tok, records):
   160	    """Per record: whether each successful call used a use line (from the saved program heads)."""
   161	    out = []
   162	    for r in records:
   163	        out.append([("\nuse " in (c.get("program_head") or "")) for c in r["calls"] if not c.get("error")])
   164	    return out
     1	"""
     2	Registered GRPO reward for r4 (R4.md, before any RL spend).
     3	
     4	    1.0    the final answer is correct and the response ended
     5	    +0.25  times the fraction of reference rows present in the written tables
     6	           (tape: (state, event, destination) names; number mode: the 5-token row).
     7	           0 when the prompt has no reference table
     8	    -0.2   per `identical to call` error
     9	    -len_cost per 1,000 generated tokens (0.01, PROMPTBOX's value)
    10	    +0.1   if any call succeeds after an error
    11	
    12	Arm B's learning rate is the unique constant that makes the cosine schedule sum to 15 times PROMPTBOX's
    13	summed learning rate (60 steps at 1e-5).
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import math
    19	
    20	
    21	PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR = 60 * 1e-5
    22	
    23	
    24	def _multiplier(step, total, warmup):
    25	    if step < warmup:
    26	        return (step + 1) / warmup
    27	    frac = (step - warmup) / max(1, total - warmup)
    28	    return 0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * frac))
    29	
    30	
    31	def arm_b_lr(total=80, warmup=0, multiple=15.0):
    32	    """Learning rate whose schedule sums to `multiple` times PROMPTBOX's summed lr."""
    33	    scale = sum(_multiplier(s, total, warmup) for s in range(total))
    34	    return multiple * PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR / scale
    35	
    36	
    37	def schedule_sum(lr, total=80, warmup=0):
    38	    return sum(lr * _multiplier(s, total, warmup) for s in range(total))
    39	
    40	
    41	def score(correct, finished, n_tokens, n_identical, recovered, row_frac, len_cost=0.01):
    42	    value = 1.0 if correct and finished else 0.0
    43	    value += 0.25 * row_frac
    44	    value -= 0.2 * n_identical
    45	    value -= len_cost * n_tokens / 1000.0
    46	    value += 0.1 if recovered else 0.0
    47	    return value
    48	
    49	
    50	def name_rows(tok, prog):
    51	    """Written rows as name tuples. Tape rows are 3 names; number-mode rows are 5."""
    52	    out = []
    53	    for reg in prog.get("registers") or []:
    54	        width = 5 if reg.get("kind") == "mealy" else 3
    55	        for row in reg.get("rows") or []:
    56	            if len(row) < width:
    57	                continue
    58	            out.append(tuple(tok.decode([t]).strip() for t in row[:width]))
    59	    return out
    60	
    61	
    62	def row_fraction(reference, written):
    63	    if not reference:
    64	        return 0.0
    65	    got = set(written)
    66	    return sum(1 for row in reference if tuple(row) in got) / len(reference)
    67	
    68	
    69	def reference_rows(ex):
    70	    """Gold table rows from a composite example. Empty when the prompt has no reference table."""
    71	    if not ex:
    72	        return []
    73	    rows = []
    74	    for kind, spec in ex.get("segments") or []:
    75	        if kind != "call":
    76	            continue
    77	        for reg in ((spec or {}).get("lit") or {}).get("registers") or []:
    78	            rows.extend(tuple(row) for row in reg.get("rows") or [])
    79	    return rows
    80	
    81	
    82	def selftest():
    83	    assert abs(schedule_sum(arm_b_lr()) - 15 * PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR) < 1e-12
    84	    assert abs(40 * 3e-4 - 20 * PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR) < 1e-12

I also found two RL discrepancies to check against the frozen text: the “KL rehearsal” uses response cross-entropy, and numeric reference calls appear to contribute no rows to the row reward.

+def classify_number_line(text):
+    """("result", k) | ("number", k) | ("quote", before, after) | ("bad", text) | None if it is not a number line."""
+    m = RESULT_LINE_RE.match(text)
+    if m:
+        return ("result", int(m.group(2)))
+    m = NUMBER_INDEX_RE.match(text)
+    if m:
+        return ("number", int(m.group(2)))
+    m = A.NUMBER_LINE_RE.match(text)
+    if m:
+        try:
+            return ("quote", json.loads(m.group(2)), json.loads(m.group(3)))
+        except ValueError:
+            return ("bad", text)
+    if text.startswith(("first:", "second:")):
+        return ("bad", text)
+    return None
+
+
+def citation_message(which, raw, n_ok):
+    """What to write instead of a malformed number line. n_ok is how many calls have run."""
+    body = raw.split(":", 1)[-1].strip()
+    if re.search(r"\bresult\b", body, re.I):
+        if n_ok <= 0:
+            return f"{which}: write result <k> only after a call has run; none has"
+        if n_ok == 1:
+            return f"{which}: write result <k>, k = 1"
+        if n_ok == 2:
+            return f"{which}: write result <k>, k = 1 or 2"
+        return f"{which}: write result <k>, k = 1 or 2 or … {n_ok}"
+    return f"{which}: write `result k` or a quote of the number; got {body[:80]!r}"
+
+
+def rowish(tok, line):
+    """A line of short name tokens, not prose (so an answer line is not mistaken for a row)."""
+    parts = [tok.decode([x]) for x in line]
+    return all("\n" not in p and " " not in p.strip() and 0 < len(p.strip()) <= 12 for p in parts)
+
+
+def row_width_message(reg, n_tokens):
+    """Moore header over Mealy rows, or the reverse. None if this is not that mismatch."""
+    name = reg["name"]
+    if n_tokens == 5 and reg["kind"] == "moore":
+        return f"register {name}: rows have 5 tokens but the header has no (output …)"
+    if n_tokens == 3 and reg["kind"] == "mealy":
+        return f"register {name}: rows have 3 tokens but the header has (output {reg.get('output')})"
+    return None
+
+
+def list_signature(notes):
+    """Comma-separated list lines in the notes, labels stripped. Prose with a comma is not a list line."""
+    body = FINAL_BLOCK_RE.sub("", notes)
+    lines = []
+    for ln in body.splitlines():
+        ln = ln.strip().rstrip(".")
+        if "," not in ln:
+            continue
+        payload = ln.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in ln else ln
+        parts = [p.strip() for p in payload.split(",")]
+        if len(parts) >= 2 and all(p and " " not in p for p in parts):
+            lines.append(payload.strip())
+    return tuple(lines)
+
+
+def repeat_refusal(text, results):
+    """`identical to call k` when this program block equals an earlier failed call and the notes' list lines
+    are unchanged. A new list line is a real notes repair and is allowed; a prose sentence is not."""
+    matches = list(PROGRAM_BLOCK_RE.finditer(text))
+    if len(matches) < 2:
+        return None
+    cur = matches[-1]
+    cur_sig = list_signature(notes_of(text[:cur.end()]))
+    cur_prog = cur.group(0)
+    for i, m in enumerate(matches[:-1]):
+        if i >= len(results) or results[i] is not None:
+            continue
+        if m.group(0) != cur_prog:
+            continue
+        if list_signature(notes_of(text[:m.end()])) == cur_sig:
+            return f"identical to call {i + 1}"
+    return None
+
+
+def _nearest(source, quote, i):
+    """A same-length slice of source at or after i that is closest to quote, or None."""
+    import difflib
+    quote = quote[:80]
+    if not quote or i >= len(source):
+        return None
+    ch, pos, p = quote[0], [], i
+    while len(pos) < 250:
+        j = source.find(ch, p)
+        if j < 0:
+            break
+        pos.append(j)
+        p = j + 1
+    best, best_r = None, 0.0
+    n = len(quote)
+    for j in pos:
+        sl = source[j:j + n]
+        if len(sl) < max(1, n // 2):
+            continue
+        r = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, quote, sl).ratio()
+        if r > best_r:
+            best_r, best = r, sl
+    return best if best_r >= 0.45 else None
+
+
+def explain_locate(sources, before, after, frm, label):
+    """An anchors-not-found line that names the quote that failed and the nearest text."""
+    k0, i0 = frm
+    failed, where, near = (before or after), "in the question or the notes", None
+    if frm != (0, 0):
+        where = "after the previous number"
+    for k in range(k0, len(sources)):
+        i = i0 if k == k0 else 0
+        opened = True if before == "" else match(sources[k], before, i)
+        if before != "" and opened is None:
+            failed, where = before, where
+            near = _nearest(sources[k], before, i)
+            if near:
+                break
+            continue
+        if after == "":
+            continue
+        start = i if before == "" else opened[1]
+        if match(sources[k], after, start) is None:
+            failed, where = after, "after the opening quote"
+            near = _nearest(sources[k], after, start)
+            break
+    near_s = "" if not near else f"; nearest: {_q(near)}"
+    return f"{label}: {_q(failed)} does not occur {where}{near_s}"
+
+
+def span_tokens(tok, source, span):
+    """Non-separator tokens wholly inside the span, in order."""
+    enc = tok.encode(source, add_special_tokens=False)
+    a, b = span
+    out = []
+    for (s, e) in enc.offsets:
+        raw = source[s:e]
+        s2 = s + len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip())
+        e2 = e - (len(raw) - len(raw.rstrip()))
+        if e2 <= a or s2 >= b or s2 < a or e2 > b:
+            continue
+        text = source[s2:e2]
+        if text and text not in pb_lit.SEPARATORS:
+            out.append(text)
+    return out
+

    58	def loguniform_int(rng, lo, hi):
    59	    return int(round(math.exp(rng.uniform(math.log(lo), math.log(hi)))))
    60	
    61	
    62	def random_number(rng, n):
    63	    if n == 1:
    64	        return str(rng.randint(0, 9))
    65	    return str(rng.randint(1, 9)) + "".join(rng.choice(A.DIGITS) for _ in range(n - 1))

    68	def pick_phrasing(rng, split, n=N_PHRASINGS, held=N_HELD, phrasing=None):
    69	    """train: one of the first n − held phrasings; test (held-out phrasing of a training template): one of the last."""
    70	    if phrasing is not None:
    71	        return phrasing
    72	    return rng.randrange(n - held) if split == "train" else n - held + rng.randrange(held)
    73

    75	def _c(rng, xs):
   190	def number_spec(op, q, refs):
   191	    """refs: two of ("span", (start, end)) | ("result", k)."""
   192	    out, frm = [], 0
   193	    for ref in refs:
   194	        if ref[0] == "result":
   195	            out.append(("result", ref[1]))
   196	            continue
   197	        qq = number_quote(q, ref[1], frm)
   198	        if qq is None:
   199	            qq = number_quote(q, ref[1], 0)
   200	        if qq is None:
   201	            return None
   202	        out.append(("quote", qq[0], qq[1]))
   203	        frm = ref[1][1]
   204	    return {"kind": "numbers", "op": op, "first": out[0], "second": out[1]}
   205	
   206	
   207	def spec_ids(tok, spec):
   208	    if spec["kind"] == "tape":
   209	        return R.tape_program_ids(tok, spec["lit"])
   210	    if spec["first"][0] == "raw" or spec["second"][0] == "raw":
   211	        # a malformed number line (only in faulty calls): the program text with that line as written
   212	        text = R.number_program_text(spec["op"], *(("result", 0) if r[0] == "raw" else r
   213	                                                    for r in (spec["first"], spec["second"])))
   214	        for which, r in (("first", spec["first"]), ("second", spec["second"])):
   215	            if r[0] == "raw":
   216	                text = text.replace(f"{which}: result 0\n", f"{which}: {r[1]}\n")
   217	        return R.enc(tok, text)
   218	    return R.number_program_ids(tok, spec["op"], spec["first"], spec["second"])
   219

   221	def ref_of(spec, truth):
   222	    """The reference call for scoring: tables (tape) or op and numbers (number mode), and the true reading."""
   803	def parking_fee(tok, rng, L, split="test", phrasing=None):
   804	    cap = rng.randint(3, 9)
   805	    s0 = rng.randint(0, cap)
   806	    items = [_c(rng, ["E", "X"]) for _ in range(L)]
   807	    n = int(run_rows(counter_rows(cap, "E", "X"), str(s0), items))
   808	    p = rng.randint(2, 20)
   809	    i = rng.randrange(3) if phrasing is None else phrasing
   810	    Q = [
   811	        f"A car park with {cap} spaces opens with {s0} cars inside. Gate log (E = a car enters, X = a car leaves): "
   812	        f"{{lst}}. A car that arrives when the park is full drives away, and an X when it is empty is a sensor "
   813	        f"glitch and is ignored. At closing, every parked car pays {p} dollars. How much is collected at closing?",
   814	        f"Parking garage: {cap} spots, {s0} occupied at opening. Sensor events: {{lst}} (E entry, X exit; entries "
   815	        f"when full are turned away and exits when empty are ignored). Overnight fee: {p} dollars per car still "
   816	        f"parked. What are the total overnight fees?",
   817	        f"The lot fits {cap} cars and starts with {s0}. Events: {{lst}}. E means one car in (unless full), X one car "
   818	        f"out (unless empty). The cars left at the end pay {p} dollars each. What is the total they pay?",
   819	    ]
   820	    q, sp = compose(Q[i], lst=_list(rng, items))
   821	    spec = tape_spec(q, sp["lst"][0], "cars in the car park after the log", "the gate events in order",
   822	                     [("n", str(s0), counter_rows(cap, "E", "X"))], "{n} cars are parked.")
   823	    if spec is None:
   824	        return None
   825	    ans = n * p
   826	    segs = [("call", spec), ("text", f"{n} cars remain, paying {p} dollars each: {n} × {p} = {ans}.\n\nAnswer: {ans}")]
   827	    return _out(q, segs, ans, "number", L, [spec], [items], [str(n)], i)

   830	def four_total(tok, rng, L, split="test", phrasing=None):
   831	    a, b, c, d = (random_number(rng, n) for n in _lengths(rng, L, 4))
   832	    i = rng.randrange(3) if phrasing is None else phrasing
   833	    Q = [
   834	        "Four warehouses hold {a}, {b}, {c} and {d} units. How many units are there in all four together?",
   835	        "A census counted {a} people in the first region, {b} in the second, {c} in the third and {d} in the "
   836	        "fourth. What is the total population of the four regions?",
   837	        "Yearly revenue in cents, by quarter: {a} (first), {b} (second), {c} (third), {d} (fourth). What is the "
   838	        "total for the year in cents?",
   839	    ]
   840	    q, sp = compose(Q[i], a=a, b=b, c=c, d=d)
   841	    s1, s2 = int(a) + int(b), int(c) + int(d)
   842	    c1 = number_spec("add", q, [("span", sp["a"][0]), ("span", sp["b"][0])])
   843	    c2 = number_spec("add", q, [("span", sp["c"][0]), ("span", sp["d"][0])])
   844	    c3 = number_spec("add", q, [("result", 1), ("result", 2)])
   845	    if None in (c1, c2, c3):
   846	        return None
   847	    tot = s1 + s2
   848	    segs = [("call", c1), ("call", c2), ("call", c3), ("text", f"Answer: {tot}")]
   849	    return _out(q, segs, tot, "number", L, [c1, c2, c3], [[a, b], [c, d], [str(s1), str(s2)]],
   850	                [str(s1), str(s2), str(tot)], i)

   853	def coin_add_or_sub(tok, rng, L, split="test", phrasing=None, digits=None):
   854	    items = [_c(rng, ["H", "T"]) for _ in range(L)]
   855	    D = digits or loguniform_int(rng, 5, 300)
   856	    a = random_number(rng, D)
   857	    b = random_number(rng, max(1, D - rng.randint(0, 2)))
   858	    if int(b) > int(a):
   859	        a, b = b, a
   860	    even = items.count("H") % 2 == 0
   861	    i = rng.randrange(3) if phrasing is None else phrasing
   862	    Q = [
   863	        "Coin tosses: {lst}. If the number of heads (H) is even, add {a} and {b}; if it is odd, subtract {b2} from "
   864	        "{a2}. What is the result?",
   865	        "A referee tossed a coin: {lst}. An even count of H means the score is {a} plus {b}; an odd count means "
   866	        "{a2} minus {b2}. What is the score?",
   867	        "Flip record {lst}. Even heads: {a} + {b}. Odd heads: {a2} − {b2}. Give the number.",
   868	    ]
   869	    q, sp = compose(Q[i], lst=_list(rng, items), a=a, b=b, a2=a, b2=b)
   870	    s1 = tape_spec(q, sp["lst"][0], "parity of the number of heads", "the tosses in order",
   871	                   [("p", "even", parity_rows("H", "T"))], "Heads: {p}.")
   872	    if even:
   873	        s2 = number_spec("add", q, [("span", sp["a"][0]), ("span", sp["b"][0])])
   874	        ans = int(a) + int(b)
   875	    else:
   876	        s2 = number_spec("sub", q, [("span", sp["a2"][0]), ("span", sp["b2"][0])])
   877	        ans = int(a) - int(b)
   878	    if None in (s1, s2):
   879	        return None
   880	    segs = [("call", s1), ("text", ""), ("call", s2), ("text", f"Answer: {ans}")]
   881	    return _out(q, segs, ans, "number", L, [s1, s2], [items, [a, b]], ["even" if even else "odd", str(ans)], i,
   882	                digits=D)

     9	- Sentence mode stays out. SEMANTIC.md §6.2 says the pilot is not clearly positive: held-out templates were 1/18, and the line skipped its counted eval.
    10	- RegVM stays out. A regvm call is a third program form (`machine: regvm`). The program-writing pilot was revised after seeing which rules failed. That is not a simple addition to REASON's calls.
    11	- There is no `events:` alphabet line. SEMANTIC_FORMAT already uses `events:` for sentence spans. The alphabet is taught by compile's families and by the walk error, which names the table's events.
    12	- `use` lines are in. `compile_core` expands them; a program without one parses as before (REASON_FORMAT §12).
    13	- `first: number k` / `second: number k` are in. Quotes and `result k` stay valid and stay in the training mix.
    14	- An identical resubmission is the same program block as an earlier failed call, with the same notes list-signature. A new comma-separated list line is a notes repair and is allowed. The rule is in REASON_FORMAT §12.
    15	- The eval generation budget stays 12,000 tokens per round, the harness value REASON published, so the comparison is paired.
    16	- R1 keeps parking and the other five held-out templates, including the clipped counters.

    21	
    22	Every training example is built through `reason_core.build` and kept only when the reference calls match Python.

    28	- Executor: 16 pinned tables and 16 Mealy tables every step, a subtracter on 15% of the Mealy draws.
    29	- Language: ordinary 0.25, general 0.20, negarith 0.04, KL coefficient 1.0.

    57	
    58	Run only if both of these hold on the selected checkpoint, and the cap still covers a re-eval of R1 (clock) plus a language eval:
    59	
    60	1. Selected accuracy on the 48 is at least 0.08 above reason-s1 on those same 48.
    61	2. Language screen, read from `research/reason/results/lm-s1/lm_reason-s1-500.json` and the new `pb_lmeval` JSON: GSM8K rate at least the reason-s1 rate minus 0.04, GSM8K program rate at most 0.05, MMLU rate at least the reason-s1 rate minus 0.015.
    62	
    63	If the gate fails, the reported model is the selected SFT adapter.
    64	
    65	PROMPTBOX's summed learning rate is 60 steps at 1e-5, one Adam step each, constant: 6e-4 (`r4_reward.PROMPTBOX_SUMMED_LR`).
    66	
    67	- Arm B, the arm that runs if the gate passes: 80 steps, warmup 0, cosine as in `reason_train.lr_at`. `--lr` is `r4_reward.arm_b_lr()`, the value whose schedule sums to 15 times that. Executor terms and the KL rehearsal stay on every update.
    68	- Arm C, only if B finishes, B's language screen passes, and the cap still holds a re-eval: 40 steps at a constant 3e-4 (20 times). If B fails the language screen, Arm C does not run and RL is not adopted.
    69	
    70	Reward (`r4_reward.score`): 1 if the final answer is correct and the response ended; plus 0.25 times the fraction of reference rows present in the written tables (tape rows are (state, event, destination); number-mode rows are the 5-token row; 0 if the prompt has no reference table); minus 0.2 per `identical to call` error; minus 0.01 per 1,000 generated tokens; plus 0.1 if any call succeeds after an error.

It checks the pooled k against the sum of the cells, against the records' `correct` flags, and against a second scorer (the saved answer region, the problem's full answer, `last_number` / `answer_region`). A report that does not match is not entered in this file.

   375	
   376	## What could be wrong
   377	
   378	1. A container running yesterday's sources. The image env `R4_SOURCES_SHA256` changes when the sources change, the worker overwrites `/root/pb` with the shipped files and refuses on a hash mismatch, and a job is spawned only from a clean commit. Ruled out for a job when its log shows the matching hash and the new error text.
   379	2. A merged adapter left in `/tmp` by a warm container. The worker deletes `/tmp/pb_*merged*` and `reason_loop` merges to `/tmp/pb_reason_merged_{PB_JOB_ID}`. Ruled out when the job log shows a merge into that path.
   380	3. A typed or unpaired count. `r4_report.py` is the only way a count enters this file, and the second scorer has to match. Comparisons use the same cells and the same seed. Ruled out per table when the script prints `"match": true`.
    84	def answer_key(prog, final, answer):
    85	    """What a correct plain-words answer must say: Yes/No, an option letter, a long number read off the run, or the
    86	    values the answer template prints, in order."""
    87	    case_text = answer_case(prog, final)
    88	    key = {"yesno": True if answer.startswith("Yes") else False if answer.startswith("No") else None,
    89	           "words": [], "numbers": [], "number_domains": [], "word_domains": [], "text": None}
    90	    option = re.search(r"\(([A-Z])\)", answer)
    91	    if option:
    92	        key["option"] = option.group(1)
    93	        return key
    94	    if re.search(r"\{[a-z][a-z0-9_]*<\}", case_text):
    95	        key["digits"] = re.findall(r"\d+", answer)[-1]
    96	        return key
    97	    places = {r.name: re.search(r"\{" + re.escape(r.name) + r"[}#<]", case_text) for r in prog.registers}
    98	    shown = sorted(((m.start(), r) for r in prog.registers if (m := places[r.name])), key=lambda t: t[0])
    99	    key["labels"], key["slots"] = [], []
   100	    for pos, r in shown:
   101	        v = final[r.name]
   102	        before = case_text[:pos].rstrip(" :=(").split()
   103	        label = before[-1].lower().strip(",.") if before else ""
   104	        key["labels"].append(label)
   105	        if "{" + r.name + "#}" in case_text or (r.kind == "num" and v not in r.extras):
   106	            num = r.nums[r.index(v)] if "{" + r.name + "#}" in case_text else int(v)
   107	            domain = [n for n in r.nums if n is not None]
   108	            key["numbers"].append(num)
   109	            key["number_domains"].append(domain)
   110	            key["slots"].append({"label": label, "kind": "num", "value": num, "domain": domain})
   111	        else:
   112	            key["words"].append(v)
   113	            key["word_domains"].append([x for x in r.values])
   114	            key["slots"].append({"label": label, "kind": "word", "value": v, "domain": [x for x in r.values]})
   115	    if key["yesno"] is None and not shown:
   116	        key["text"] = answer
   117	    return key
   118	
   119	
   120	def _norm(text):
   121	    return " ".join("".join(c.lower() if c.isalnum() else " " for c in text).split())
   122	
   123	
   124	def last_number(text):
   125	    """The last number written in a text, digit groups joined across commas, spaces and thin spaces."""
   126	    runs = re.findall(r"\d(?:[\d,\u2009\u202f ]*\d)?", text)
   127	    return re.sub(r"\D", "", runs[-1]) if runs else None
   128	
   129	
   130	def last_option(text):
   131	    found = re.findall(r"\(([A-Z])\)", text)
   132	    return found[-1] if found else None
   133	
   134	
   135	def answer_correct(key, text):
   136	    """Whether a plain-words answer states exactly what the key requires (and nothing contradicting it)."""
   137	    t = text.strip()
   138	    if key.get("option") is not None:
   139	        return last_option(t) == key["option"]
   140	    if key.get("digits") is not None:
   141	        return last_number(t) == key["digits"]
   142	    if key.get("text") is not None:
   143	        return _norm(t) == _norm(key["text"])
   144	    if key["yesno"] is not None:
   145	        low = t.lower().lstrip("*\"' ")
   146	        if not low.startswith("yes" if key["yesno"] else "no"):
   147	            return False
   148	    slots = key.get("slots") or []
   149	    if len(slots) == 1:
   150	        return _one_slot(slots[0], t)
   151	    if slots:
   152	        return _many_slots(slots, t)
   153	    return True
   154	
   155	
   156	def _tok(word):
   157	    """Single letters keep their case (so the article "a" is not the value "A"); words compare in lower case."""
   158	    return word if len(word) == 1 else word.lower()
   159	
   160	
   161	def _mentions(slots, t):
   162	    """Domain values the text mentions, in order, leaving out labels (a token followed by '=')."""
   163	    domain = {_tok(str(x)) for sl in slots for x in sl["domain"]}
   164	    out = []
   165	    for m in re.finditer(r"[A-Za-z]+|\d+", t):
   166	        if re.match(r"\s*=", t[m.end():]):
   167	            continue
   168	        w = _tok(m.group())
   169	        if w in domain:
   170	            out.append(w)
   171	    return out
   172	
   173	
   174	def _labelled(slot, t):
   175	    lab = slot["label"]
   176	    if not lab or not lab.isalpha():
   177	        return []
   178	    found = [_tok(x) for x in re.findall(rf"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_]){re.escape(lab)}(?![A-Za-z0-9_])\s*(?:=|:|\bis\b|\bwas\b)?"
   179	                                         r"\s*([A-Za-z]+|\d+)", t, flags=re.I)]
   180	    if slot["kind"] == "num":
   181	        return [x for x in found if x.isdigit()]
   182	    domain = {_tok(str(v)) for v in slot["domain"]}
   183	    return [x for x in found if x in domain]
   184	
   185	
   186	def _one_slot(slot, t):
   187	    want = _tok(str(slot["value"]))
   188	    if slot["kind"] == "word":
   189	        found = _mentions([slot], t)
   190	        return want in found and all(w == want for w in found)
   191	    got = _labelled(slot, t)
   192	    if got:
   193	        return got == [want]
   194	    domain = {n for n in slot["domain"]}
   195	    nums = [int(x) for x in re.findall(r"(?<![\d.])\d+(?![\d.]*\d)", t)]
   196	    return [n for n in nums if n in domain] == [slot["value"]]
   197	
   198	
   199	def _many_slots(slots, t):
   200	    want = [_tok(str(sl["value"])) for sl in slots]
   201	    labels = [sl["label"] for sl in slots]
   202	    if all(labels) and len(set(labels)) == len(labels):
   203	        got = [_labelled(sl, t) for sl in slots]
   204	        if all(got):
   205	            if any(g != [w] * len(g) for g, w in zip(got, want)):
    64	def ordinary_items(seed=0):
    65	    """(question text, format, gold, source) for every training item, formats drawn once with a fixed seed."""
    66	    from datasets import load_dataset
    67	    rng = random.Random(seed)
    68	    out = []
    69	    for r in load_dataset("openai/gsm8k", "main", split="train"):
    70	        fmt = rng.choice(list(GSM_FORMATS))
    71	        gold = r["answer"].split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")
    72	        out.append({"question": GSM_FORMATS[fmt].format(q=r["question"]), "format": fmt, "gold": gold,
    73	                    "source": "gsm8k"})
    74	    for cfg in ("ARC-Easy", "ARC-Challenge"):
    75	        for r in load_dataset("allenai/ai2_arc", cfg, split="train"):
    76	            labels = r["choices"]["label"]
    77	            if any(lab not in "ABCDE" for lab in labels) or r["answerKey"] not in labels:
    78	                continue
    79	            opts = "\n".join(f"{lab}. {c}" for lab, c in zip(labels, r["choices"]["text"]))
    80	            fmt = rng.choice(list(ARC_FORMATS))
    81	            out.append({"question": ARC_FORMATS[fmt].format(q=r["question"], opts=opts), "format": fmt,
    82	                        "gold": r["answerKey"], "source": "arc"})
    83	    return out
    84	
    85	
    86	def is_right(item, text):
    87	    if item["source"] == "gsm8k":
    88	        return same_number(number_of(text, item["format"]), item["gold"])
    89	    return letter_of(text, item["format"]) == item["gold"]
    90	
    91	
    92	def ordinary(args, llm):
    93	    from vllm import SamplingParams
    94	    items = ordinary_items()
    95	    outs = llm.generate([chat_prompt(it["question"]) for it in items],
    96	                        SamplingParams(temperature=0.7, top_p=0.8, top_k=20, max_tokens=1536, seed=3))
    97	    kept, dropped = [], {"truncated": 0, "wrong": 0}
    98	    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
    99	        text = o.outputs[0].text
   100	        if o.outputs[0].finish_reason != "stop":
   101	            dropped["truncated"] += 1
   102	            continue
   103	        if not is_right(it, text):
   104	            dropped["wrong"] += 1

   213	def render_stream(items, fmt):
   214	    if fmt == "comma":
   215	        return ", ".join(items)
   216	    if fmt == "space":
   217	        return " ".join(items)
   218	    if fmt == "lines":
   219	        return "\n".join(items)
   220	    if fmt == "concat":
   221	        return "".join(items)
   222	    if fmt == "arrow":
   223	        return " -> ".join(items)
   224	    raise ValueError(fmt)
